Fikradaha
 
 Agoosto 2026 
 
 
 
Qoraallada hoose waa diiwaan joogto ah oo fikrado iyo aragtiyo ah oo igu soo dhacayay waqti ka dib. Badanaa ma ahan kuwo si buuxda loo dhammaystiray ama loo hirgeliyay, in kasta oo ay mustaqbalka noqon karaan. Ama laga yaabee inaysan noqon, qaar ka mid ah fikradaha waxay ku filan yihiin qaabka xusuus-qorka. Qaar waa waddooyin xiran oo aan u qalmin in la sii horumariyo. Laakiin weli, waxaa laga yaabaa inay mudan yihiin in la diiwaangeliyo.
 
 2023-02-24:  KHILAFKA FILTARRADA NEERKA
 
Waxaan si lama filaan ah u ogaaday intii aan shaqaynayay in markaan isku dayo inaan sawir ku weyneeyo Photoshop anigoo isticmaalaya Neural Filters, sawirkuna uu ku jiro profile-ka midabka CMYK oo uu leeyahay qaybo hufan oo waaweyn, uu dhacayo khilaaf yaab leh, laakiin inta badan aad u qurux badan.

 2023-10-04:  JIDKA HIDDA-SIDEEDKA
 
Waxaan u beddelay taxanaha hidda-sideedka sawirro anigoo isticmaalaya xeer fudud: A waxay ka dhigan tahay tallaabo xagga woqooyi ah, T/U xagga koonfureed, C xagga bari iyo G xagga galbeed. Sidaas darteed, kala dambaynta saldhigyadu waxay go'aamisaa qaabka xariiqda isku xiran.

 2024-03-22:  RITMIGA MUUQAALKA EE SHAQADA KOMBIYUTARKA
 
Waxaan abuuray barnaamij diiwaangeliya garaac kasta oo kiiboodhka ah iyo gujinta jiirka inta aan ku guda jiro shaqadayda kombiyuutarka. Waqtigu wuxuu u socdaa jihada qoraalka caadiga ah, bidix ilaa midig iyo kor ilaa hoos. Dhibic madow waxay ka dhigan tahay gelitaan la diiwaangeliyay, meel cadna waa maqnaanshaheeda.

 2024-08-19:  MAANSADA KUSHÍK
 
Waxaan ka shaqaynayay kombiyuutarka, laakiin jiirkayga Kushík ayaa si joogto ah ii mashquulinayay. Wuxuu igu dul fuulayay, wuxuu dalbanayay dareen, wuxuu dul fuulayay miiskayga, kombiyuutarka. Wax yar ayuu bilaabay inuu i niyad-jabiyo. Markaas ayaan xanaaqay, waxaan u furay dukumeenti MS Word oo madhan, waxaana u sheegay wax la mid ah inuu hal-abuur sameeyo haddii uu doonayo inuu ii sameeyo.
 Kushík markaas wuxuu bilaabay inuu ku ordo kiiboodhka.
 
Dukumeentigan ayaa la abuuray: koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf]
 
 2024-12-05:  THERMOZEPAM
 
Waxaan u sameeyay kamaradayda kuleylka ee Seek Thermal RevealPRO barnaamijkayga gaarka ah ee lagu daawado oo lagu saxo sawirrada kuleylka. Windows-ka kama helin wax software ah oo ku habboon kamaradan, taas oo ah wax laga xumaado, sababtoo ah faylasha kuleylka waxay ka kooban yihiin xogta heerkulka oo lagu sii shaqayn karo.
 
Barnaamijka waxaan ugu magac daray Thermozepam, sababtoo ah waan ku qanacsanahay naqshaddiisa, isticmaalkiisuna wuxuu ii yahay mid balwad leh. Barnaamijku wuxuu akhriyaa faylasha TIFF ee kuleylka, wuxuu kuu oggolaanayaa inaad beddesho khariidadda midabka ee sawirka adigoo isticmaalaya colormap ka maktabadda matplotlib, cabbirka heerkulka celceliska ee sawirka, go'aaminta qaabka heerkulka, muujinta heerkulka ugu yar iyo kan ugu sarreeya, histogram, iyo ka dib gujinta sidoo kale heerkulka pixel gaar ah. Marka la kaydinayo, wuxuu awood u leeyahay inuu dhoofiyo faylka PNG ee midabka dib loo habeeyay oo ikhtiyaari ahaan u weyneeyo adigoo isticmaalaya interpolation.

 Koodhka isha: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw]
 
 2025-12-05:  IFTIIN GUDCURKA
 
Waxaan ka fikiray inaan sameeyo mashruuc Arduino ah, kaas oo aan ku ridi lahaa leysar sanduuq madhan (oo horay loogu nadiifiyay laambad UV-C ah). Leysarkaas wuxuu soo saari lahaa garaac hal ilbiriqsi ah waqti aan la garanayn (si aanan u ogaanin goorta ay si sax ah u dhacday, laakiin aan ogaado inay dhici doonto tusaale ahaan 10 daqiiqadood gudahood 100%). Haddii aan si sax ah u samayn lahaa, garaacaas iftiinka ah ma jiro qof arki lahaa.
 
 2026-01-31:  SIMULAYNTA KAMARADA PINHOLE
 
Waxaan isku dayay inaan Python ku abuuro kamarad pinhole ah oo fudud oo shaqaynaysa. Mar hore dugsigii sare waxaan horey u sameeyay mid dhab ah oo jireed, laakiin waxaan dareemay in haddii aan simuleeyo, aan si fiican u fahmi doono sida ay u shaqeyso, sababtoo ah kuma filna oo kaliya in la sameeyo dalool sanduuq ah, la geliyo warqad iftiinka xasaasi u ah, ka dibna la soo saaro ka dib soo-gaadhista oo loo daayo fiisigiska inuu shaqadiisa qabto (si kasta oo ay u shaqeyso). Halkan, liddi ku ah, fiisigiska waa in sidoo kale la simuleeyaa.
 Waxaan isku dayay inaan simulaynta ka dhigo mid xisaab ahaan u macquul ah intii suurtagal ah, in kasta oo xadka uu dabcan yahay aqoontayda iyo awoodda xisaabinta ee la heli karo. Barnaamijku, tusaale ahaan, wuxuu isticmaalaa forward ray tracing, taas oo ah raadinta iftiinka laga bilaabo isha ilaa goobta iyo kamarada, halkii laga isticmaali lahaa backward ray tracing-ka caadiga ah, kaas oo raadraaca falaaraha laga bilaabo kamarada dib ugu noqoshada goobta. Habka hore wuxuu u dhow yahay jihada jireed ee faafitaanka iftiinka, laakiin badanaa waa mid aan waxtar lahayn xagga xisaabinta.
 
Tusaalooyinka dejinta barnaamijka iyo sharraxaadda cabbirrada: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt]
 Koodhka isha: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py]
 
 2026-03-02:  KALA SOOCIDDA PIXEL-KA SAWIRRADA
 
Waxaan isku dayay inaan u kala saaro pixels-ka sawirrada anigoo isticmaalaya algorithm. Fikradda waxay ahayd in pixels-ka loo kala saaro si qiyaas ah iyadoo loo eegayo HUE iyo iftiinka: hal dhidib (tusaale x) in loo kala saaro pixels-ka iyadoo loo eegayo HUE iyo dhidibka kale (tusaale y) iyadoo loo eegayo iftiinka. Tani waxay dib u habayn ku samaynaysaa pixels-ka sawirka, laakiin midna ma baaba'ayo mana jiro mid cusub oo soo baxaya. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, sawirrada soo baxaya waxay lumiyaan macnihii asalka ahaa, laakiin waxay ilaashadaan jawiga midabka.
 
Koodhka isha: koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py]
 
 2026-04-13:  BAARIDDA INTERNETKA EE LAMA FILAANKA AH
 
Waxaan qiyaastii laba toddobaad u huray baaritaanka internetka ee lama filaanka ah, sababtoo ah waxaan xiisaynayay sida uu u eg yahay marka aysan algorithms-ku kugula talin. Waxaan isku dayay habab kala duwan.
 
Midka koowaad (naiv) wuxuu ahaa abuurista barnaamij lagu geliyo oo lagu furo URL-yo si aan kala sooc lahayn loo soo saaray. Taasi dabcan waxay ahayd mid aan waxtar lahayn, booska URL-yada suurtagalka ah waa mid aad u weyn kuwa jira ayaana si yar loo matalaa.
 
Habka labaad wuxuu ahaa inaan dhisto crawler-kayga oo aan siiyo URL abuur ah oo qariib ah (tusaale shabakadda kdomivolal.cz [https://www.kdomivolal.cz/]). Natiijooyinka ayaan markaas isku dayay inaan ku sifeeyo LLM ka dibna aan daawado oo kaliya kuwa xiisaha leh (sida uu qabo qaar ka mid ah shuruudaha la qeexay). Waxaan helay waxyaabo badan oo qariib ah oo lagu maaweelo, laakiin ma jiro wax kacaan ah.
 
Habka saddexaad wuxuu ahaa mid aad u miro dhal ah oo xiiso badan. Waxaan ogaaday inuu jiro Certstream. Waa adeeg caqli badan oo si toos ah u baahiya shahaadooyinka amniga ee hadda la soo saaray adduunka oo dhan. Markaa waxay muujinaysaa URL-yada shabakadaha ama kuwa cusub ama kuwa cusboonaysiinaya shahaadadooda.
 
Koodhka isha: koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py]
 
Waxaan ku qaatay dhowr maalmood inaan si aan kala sooc lahayn u gujiyo URL-yadan. Inta badan waxaan la kulmay khiyaamo (cryptocurrency iwm.), porno, AI, AI porno, tilmaamo ku saabsan sida loo sameeyo AI porno, khamaar, is-dhexgal gelitaan oo nidaamyo ah, shabakado dhalanaya (kuwaas oo leh waxyaabo placeholder ah ama fariimo "waan ku shaqaynaynaa").
 
Nidaamkan awgiis, waxaan la kulmay shabakadda treeyo.my [https://treeyo.my/], taas oo maskaxdayda ku hartay qaabkeeda minimalist-ka ah iyo eber-ka hagaajinta dareenka daawadaha. Sidoo kale waxaan ogaaday in shabakaddaasi ay tahay qayb dhab ah oo ka mid ah internetka oo xad-dhaaf ah, sababtoo ah Google weli ma tusin. Internet Archive-ku wuu helay, laakiin wuxuu ka helay CT logs, sida anigoo kale. Raadinta ayaa ugu dambeyntii bixisay.
 
 2026-05-06:  BANDHIGGA PACIFIC-KA
 
Waxaan ka fikiray inaan abuuro 360° sawirro panoramic ah oo Pacific-ka ah oo ay soo saartay AI. Ka dibna geli QR code iyaga oo isticmaalaya AI (wax la mid ah Illusion Diffusion), kaas oo tilmaamaya farshaxan qaar. Ka dibna waxaa suurtagal noqon lahayd in la geliyo metadata been abuur ah oo goobta ah (tusaale GPS coordinates oo ku yaal bartamaha Pacific-ka) oo la geliyo Google Maps.
 
Beddel ahaan, waxaa suurtagal noqon lahayd in la qaato meelo dhab ah oo lagu sawiray 360° kamarad, tusaale ahaan hal magaalo, ka dibna dhammaantood loo beddelo boosaskooda GPS-ka ee Pacific-ka. Sababta aan uga hadlayo oo kaliya 360° sawirro waa in Google uu ilaa hadda u oggolaanayo inay u soo geliyaan boosaska GPS-ka ee saxda ah iyadoo la isticmaalayo metadata. Halka sawirrada caadiga ah ay tahay in loo qoondeeyo meel jirta.
 
 2026-06-04:  NABARADA PIGMENT-KA
 
Mashruucan waxaan isku dayay inaan hirgeliyo, laakiin sababo la xiriira waqti badan oo uu u baahan yahay, ma aanan dhammaystirin. Waxaan ilaa hadda diiwaangeliyay oo kaliya gacmaha, waxaan jaray nabarada la sawiray (background hufan), waxaan ilaaliyay cabbirkooda, waxaanan abuuray barnaamij lagu kala soocayo shabag. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, kani waa qoraalkii ay ahayd inuu sharxo fikradda:
 
“Sannadkii 2026 waxaan diiwaangeliyay buro kasta oo maqaarka ah ama cillad xididka ah oo ka muuqata dusha jidhkayga. Shay kasta cabbirkiisa waa la ilaaliyay. Ka dib, nabarada gaarka ah ayaa si dhijitaal ah loo jaray oo loo kala soocay shabag joogto ah.”
 
 2026-06-21:  BARNAAMIJKA IS-BEDDELKA AH EE CAQLIGA LEH
 
Waxaan isku dayay inaan abuuro barnaamij isticmaala OpenAI API iyo LLMs-kooda. Barnaamijku wuxuu leeyahay hawsha ah inuu wax ka beddelo naftiisa ka dibna uu mar kale is-bilaabo. Waxaana xiisaynayay meesha ay taasi u horseedi doonto.
 Nasiib darro, moodelku inta badan wuu diiday hawsha, sababtoo ah wuxuu u arkay inay khatar tahay. Ama wuu fuliyay, laakiin kii ku xigay ma uusan fulin. Attractor-ku wuxuu ahaa mid si fudud u diidaya sababo la xiriira amnigooda. Xitaa marna ma gaarin iterashadii afraad.
 
Koodhka isha: koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py]
 
 2026-07-30:  NIDAAMKA QAYBO GAAR AH
 
Nidaamkeena fiisigiska waa mid caajis ah macnaha ah inay u muuqato in qaybaha aasaasiga ah ay isku mid yihiin. Elektaroonka sida elektaroonka, foton-ka sida foton-ka.
 
Laakiin waxaan jeclaan lahaa inaan abuuro simulayn nidaam leh qaybo badan oo aasaasi ah, laakiin aan laba ka mid ah la isku beddeli karin. Ugu dambeyntii, waxaan imid nooc aan qaybuhu laftoodu lahayn wax sifooyin ah ama aqoonsi ah. Waxaa lagu qeexay oo kaliya isdhexgalkooda, taas oo marka laga eego dhinaca daawadaha gudaha nidaamka ay si dhab ah u dhiganto.
 
Cawaaqibka fikraddan waan ku raaxaystaa inaan qiyaaso. Markaan si fudud u qaato, ku noolaanshaha nidaam noocaas ah waxay noqon lahayd mid aad u adag in la sameeyo saadaalin. Sidoo kale, hay'adaha ku nool nidaam noocaas ah waxaa laga yaabaa inay u baahdaan inay ka taxadaraan luminta ama xatooyada qaybahooda.
 
Waxaan isku dayay inaan simuleeyo, farsamo ahaan waan fuliyay hawsha. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, natiijadu ma ahayn mid aad u xiiso badan. Sida aan u arko, sidoo kale waa sababtoo ah daawashada qaar ka mid ah sifooyinka soo baxaya ee xiisaha leh waxay u baahan yihiin awood xisaabinta oo weyn (qaybo badan, waqti badan oo simulayn ah, laga yaabee cabbirro badan oo simulayn ah) iyo falanqayn qoto dheer oo ku saabsan wax soo saarka.
 
Cusboonaysiinta laga bilaabo 2026-08-22: Markaan dhex marayay adduunyada la soo saaray, waxaan ugu dambeyntii la kulmay qaab-dhismeedyo is-habaynaya oo cajiib ah. Waxay yihiin kuwo wareegsan ilaa oval ah waxayna u eg yihiin xoogaa sida kaabsulka lipid-ka. Waxay leeyihiin bannaanka iyo gudaha oo si cad loo qeexay, halka caqabadda ama "membrana"-da, oo samaysa bannaankooda, ay aad u deggan tahay, halka gudahoodu uu yahay mid dhaqdhaqaaq badan. Seed-kan waxaan u dejiyay sidii qiimaha bilowga ah ee demo-ga hoose.
 
Fiiro gaar ah: horumarinta nidaamka, iyo sidaas darteed cabbirka qaab-dhismeedyadan, waxay ku xiran tahay xallinta muuqaalka. Xawaaraha animation-ka wuxuu saameeyaa xasilloonidooda, malaha sababtoo ah saxnaanta tirooyinka marka la eego tallaabooyinka waqtiga ee waaweyn. Xitaa haddii qaab-dhismeedyadu ay burburaan, tusaale ahaan ka dib markii la kordhiyo xawaaraha animation-ka, nidaamku ugu dambeyntii wuu is-habaynayaa mar kale wuxuuna abuuri doonaa kuwo cusub.
 
Tani ma ahan wax aan damacsanaa inaan abuuro. Waxaan abuuray oo kaliya nidaam leh částicemi/interakcemi gaar ah tanina waxay u soo baxday sidii ifafaale soo baxaya. Waa wax cajiib ah, si lama filaan ah waxaan u haystaa simulaytor "lipid-yo" ah.
 
Demo is-dhexgal ah: koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html]
 
 2026-08-14:  QASHIN
 
Maxaa dhici lahaa haddii aan sawiro qashin kasta oo aan ku rido qashinka? Waxaa suurtagal ah in lagu sawiro cabbir, ka dibna qashinka laga gooyo asalka, oo lagu habeeyo shabakad joogto ah oo leh saamiga cabbirka ee u dhigma.
 
 2026-08-22: 天天中彩票买 
 
Markii aan la tashaday ChatGPT (v5.6 Sol) oo ku saabsan sida ugu fudud ee loo qeexi karo tijaabada RITMIGA OPTICAL EE SHAQADA EE KOMBIUTARKA (eeg kor), waxay si toos ah u magacawday sheekada "Sharaxaadda barnaamijka  天天中彩票买 ". Isla mar ahaantaana, sheekadu waxay ahayd mid cusub oo loo sameeyay oo keliya si loo sharxo tijaabadan. Nuxurka barnaamijka ee aan u diray iyada wuxuu ahaa mid gebi ahaanba caadi ah.
 
Markaa waxaan go'aansaday inaan kala hadlo arrintaas oo aan qoro tan:
 
"Maxaad sheekadan ugu magacawday Sharaxaadda barnaamijka  天天中彩票买 ?"
 
Jawaabteedu waxay ahayd:
 
"Ma garanayo. 😭 Calaamadda "զ" waa xarafka Armeeniyaanka ah ee "za", markaa "Sharaxaadda barnaamijka զ" macno uma samaynayso luuqadda Czech gabi ahaanba. Magacyada sheekadu waxay si toos ah uga soo baxaan bilowga wada hadalka, halkanna matoorka cinwaanka ayaa si cad u go'aansaday inuu ku daro xaraf aan kala sooc lahayn oo ka yimid alifbeetada Armeeniyaanka. Nuxurka wada hadalka, uma arko wax sabab ah. Markaa maya, ma aha calaamad sir ah oo loogu talagalay barnaamijka. Waa uun "Sharaxaadda barnaamijka" + far-qoraal soo galooti ah."
 
Ka dib dhawr is-dhexgal oo kale, waxay garatay in maya, aysan ahayn Armeeniyaan, laakiin ay tahay Shiineys. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, waxaa la arki karaa inay markii hore aad u wareertay. Waxaan u malaynayaa in xadhigga " 天天中彩票买 " uu u shaqeeyo wax yar sida calaamadda caanka ah ee " SolidGoldMagikarp ". Ma dhihi doono inay si isku mid ah u shaqeyso, mana garanayo haddii tokenizer-ku uu u matalo xadhigga " 天天中彩票买 " hal calaamad ahaan. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, marka la shaqaynayo, qaabku wuxuu u dhaqmaa wax yar sida ChatGPT 3 oo leh calaamadda " SolidGoldMagikarp ". Sidoo kale lagama saari karo inay la xiriirto xusuusteeda, sababtoo ah waxay gaartaa meel ka baxsan sheekadan.
 
Dhammaan sheekadan oo loo dhoofiyay PDF: koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf]
 
 2026-08-26:  BADHANKA RIYADAYDA
 
Waxaan si joogto ah uga xanaaqaa daaqadaha wada-hadalka ee nidaamka Windows, kuwaas oo bixiya waxyaabo ay ka mid yihiin kaydinta OneDrive, cusbooneysiinta, iyo wixii la mid ah. Waxay had iyo jeer bixiyaan doorasho u dhaxaysa "Bilow" iyo "Xusuusi mar dambe". Taasi waa wax laga xumaado marka aanan xiisayn midkoodna fursadahaas.
 
Markaa waxaan la imid fikrad ah inaan abuuro sawir (photomontage) oo aan ku dari doono ikhtiyaar saddexaad - badhanka riyadayda, kaas oo nasiib darro weli ka maqan nidaamka. Waxay noqon lahayd badhan "Ka tag meeshaas". Waxaan ku sharxayaa inaanan ahayn qofka kaliya ee sawirkani uu si qoto dheer u taaban doono.
 
 2026-09-18:  XISAABIYE SUPER-CAQLI AH
 
Waxaan la imid doqonnimo kale oo aan wax ku ool ahayn oo aan dareemayo inaan si aragti ahaan ah u baaro.
 
Waa barnaamij u shaqayn doona sidii xisaabiye aad u fudud. Waxay awoodi kartaa inay si fudud u darto tirooyin togan oo dhan, wax kale ma aha. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, farqiga u dhexeeya ayaa ah in natiijada aan lagu xisaabin doonin si caadi ah oo maxalli ah, laakiin halkii loo diri doono qaab hadal oo aad u horumarsan (tusaale ChatGPT-6 Astra). Waana inuu xisaabiyaa tusaalahaas fudud oo uu soo celiyo natiijada oo keliya.
 
Waxay ii muuqataa mid aad u qosol badan in la isticmaalo qaabka hadalka ee ugu horumarsan wax aad u fudud. Asal ahaan, waxay u badan tahay inay tahay parodi ku saabsan sida xitaa waxyaabaha fudud loo aamino AI maanta iyo sida suuq-geyntu u saarto calaamadda "AI" wax kasta sida nooc ka mid ah qurxinta sixirka ee shaqeynta.
 
Si dhab ah, dabcan, waxay noqon doontaa mid aad uga xun xisaabiyaha caadiga ah, oo aan la isku halayn karin, oo leh daahitaan weyn, qaali ah, deegaan ahaan aan saaxiibtinimo lahayn, oo u baahan xiriir internet, ...

Maaskaro
 
 Agoosto 2026 
 
 
 
Sawirrada hoose waa masky [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Layers_(digital_image_editing)#Layer_mask] lagu sameeyay Adobe Photoshop intii aan ku guda jiray shaqadayda mashaariic kala duwan. Maaskaro kasta waxaa loo kaydiyay sidii sawir gaar ah xilligii la abuuray. Bandhigga sawirradan ma aha natiijada xulasho. Haddii maaskaro la sameeyay intii lagu guda jiray shaqada, waa qayb ka mid ah. Sawirrada waxaa loo habeeyay si xiriir ah laga bilaabo kii ugu da'da weynaa ilaa kii ugu dambeeyay.

Dulin
 
 Maajo 2026

Waxaan la wadaagaa iyaga dhadhanka.

TITLE: Maqaalka Metaweb
SLUG: metawebovy-clanek
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/metawebovy-clanek/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-04-22
SOURCE: src/content/posts/metawebovy-clanek.mdx
BUILT_HTML: metawebovy-clanek/index.html
============================================================
Maqaalka Metaweb
 
 Abriil 2026 
 
 
 
MANIFESTO
 
Web-kan waa mashruuc taagan oo diiradda saaraya ilaalinta muddada dheer ee nuxurka.
 
Dhammaan nuxurka waxaa lagu kaydiyaa faylal, iyada oo aan lagu xidhin xog-ururin ama nidaamyo dibadda ah. Web-ka ka dhasha waxaa loo soo saaraa sidii wax soo saar taagan oo si fudud loo koobi karo, loo kaydin karo, loona dhisi karo mar kale.
 
Mashruucu wuxuu ku baahsan yahay nidaamyo badan oo madax-bannaan: kaydka (repositories), adeegyada kaydinta, iyo sawirro taagan (static snapshots). Tani waxay yaraynaysaa khatarta luminta xogta.
 
Ujeeddadu waa in la hubiyo in nuxurka dib loo dhisi karo xitaa haddii qaybo ka mid ah kaabayaashii asalka ahaa ay joojiyaan jiritaankooda.
 
LAKKAANKA KAYDINTA EE KHADKA (ONLINE)
 
Xidhiidhyadan ayaa matalaya isla mashruuca oo lagu kaydiyay nidaamyo kala duwan iyo qaabab kala duwan.
 
Waxay ku jiraan kaydka isha (canonical source repository), nuquladiisa, iyo sawirro taagan oo dhammaystiran. Si wada jir ah, waxay u abuuraan qaab-dhismeed aan loo baahnayn (redundant structure) oo loogu talagalay in lagu ilaaliyo mashruuca loona suurtageliyo dib-u-dhiskiisa xitaa haddii qaybo ka mid ah ay noqdaan kuwo aan la heli karin.
 
Mashruucu wuxuu sidoo kale u jiraa sidii xirmo Python CLI ah oo lagu qaybiyay GitHub iyo PyPI, oo ay ku jiraan sawirro fallback ah oo la geliyay oo ka mid ah farshaxannada qoraalka ee la xushay.
 
Kaydinta dhijitaalka ah ee web-kan waxay isticmaashaa, marka lagu daro domain-keeda gaarka ah, adeegyada GitHub, GitLab, Codeberg, Neocities, Cloudflare, Netlify, Vercel, Firebase, ChatGPT Sites, Arweave, Pollux.casa (Gemini), envs.net (Gemini + Gopher), Software Heritage, Internet Archive, Webarchiv, Zenodo, Figshare, Permanent.org, Proton.me, YouTube, Spotify, File Garden, Catbox, CYX Share, Ocen Share, Node 616, pOne.Rs, kappa.lol, Lulu, Kobo, PyPI, Perma.cc, archive.today, Ghost Archive, Arquivo.pt, Megalodon / Web Gyotaku iyo paste.rs.

Eeg khariidadda dhammaystiran ee kaydka (ARCHIVE.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ARCHIVE.txt]
 
DOKUMENTIGA FARSAMADA
 
Dokumentiga farsamada ee lagama maarmaanka u ah dib-u-dhiska web-kan.
 
Eeg dokumentiga farsamada ee dhammaystiran [https://vojtamaur.cz/documentation/]
 
XIRMADA ISHA EE DIB-U-DHISKA
 
Xirmada isha ee web-ka waxaa la abuuraa si toos ah inta lagu jiro dhismaha.
 
Gudaha xirmada waxaa ku jira koodhka isha ee web-kan, manifest-ka hantida, xisaabinta SHA-256 iyo qoraalka Python oo aan lahayn wax ku tiirsanaan dibadda ah. Kuma jiraan hanti waaweyn oo ka yimid public/images/, public/files/ iyo public/demos/, si cabbirka web-ka nool aan loo labanlaabin si aan loo baahnayn. Ka dib marka la furo, qoraalku wuxuu marka hore isku dayaa inuu hantida ka koobiyo gudaha dhismaha diyaarka ah ka dibna wuxuu ka soo dejistaa muraayadaha dadweynaha.
 
Sidaas darteed, koodhka isha ayaa abuura dhismaha, dhismahana wuxuu abuuraa koodhka isha.
 
Xirmada isha waa mid dib loo dhisi karo si is-daba-joog ah: ka dib marka la soo dejiyo hantida iyo rakibida ku-tiirsanaanta, web-ka iyo xirmo isha cusub ayaa dib looga dhalin karaa.
 
Soo dejiso xirmada isha ee dib-u-dhiska ee web-ka (vojtamaur-web-source.zip) [https://vojtamaur.cz/source/vojtamaur-web-source.zip]
 
DAACADNIMADA IYO AQOONSIGA DHISMAHA
 
SHA-256 waxay xaqiijinaysaa in faylasha dhismaha aysan isbeddelin. Saxiixa OpenPGP wuxuu sidoo kale kuu ogolaanayaa inaad xaqiijiso in manifest-ka xisaabinta uu saxeexay qoraaga web-kan.
 
SHA256SUMS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt] — liiska SHA-256 xisaabinta faylasha dhismaha
 SHA256SUMS.txt.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt.asc] — saxiixa OpenPGP ee goonida ah ee faylka SHA256SUMS.txt
 BUILD_SHA256.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/BUILD_SHA256.txt] — SHA-256 xisaabinta faylka SHA256SUMS.txt
 integrity.json [https://vojtamaur.cz/integrity.json] — sharraxaad macluumaad ah oo mashiinku akhrin karo oo ku saabsan daacadnimada dhismaha
 SIGNING_STATUS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SIGNING_STATUS.txt] — xaaladda macluumaadka ee saxiixa dhismaha gaarka ah
 vojta-maur-openpgp.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/keys/vojta-maur-openpgp.asc] — furaha OpenPGP ee dadweynaha si loo xaqiijiyo saxiixa
 
 Fingerprint-ka furaha OpenPGP ee ugu weyn:
 57E9 D455 FB10 A228 F66E 18AE C5F5 B390 5220 BE59 
 
Deployment kasta uma baahna inuu saxeexnaado. Dhismaha saxeexan waa inuu ku jiraa faylka SHA256SUMS.txt.asc iyo saxiixiisu waa inuu si guul leh u xaqiijiyo. Sheegashada lafteeda ee integrity.json ama SIGNING_STATUS.txt maaha caddayn saxiix sax ah; faylashan waa kuwo macluumaad uun ah.
 
DHOOFINTA (EXPORTS)
 
Web-ku wuxuu abuuraa dhoofin plain-text ah oo dhammaan maqaallada ah mar kasta oo la dhiso. Waxay ka kooban tahay nuxurka qoraalka ee qoraallada qaab toosan iyada oo aan lagu xidhin CSS, JavaScript, ama teknoolajiyada web-ka ee gaarka ah. Waxay u adeegtaa sidii lakab kaydinta ugu yar ee tusmaynta, akhrinta offline-ka, raadinta qoraalka buuxa, iyo dib-u-dhiska muddada dheer ee nuxurka xitaa haddii ay dhacdo burbur qayb ka mid ah qaab-dhismeedka asalka ah ee web-ka. Dhoofinta qoraalka ayaa sidoo kale ku habboon moodooyinka luqadda waaweyn.
 
Eeg dhoofinta qoraalka ee dhammaan maqaallada (ALL_POSTS.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ALL_POSTS.txt]
 
Habaynta mashiinka, waxaa jira dhoofin JSON-LD ah oo gacanta lagu bilaabo, taas oo ay ku jiraan qoraallada Czech iyo Ingiriisiga ee maqaallada, metadata, iyo xidhiidhada warbaahinta. Waxay isticmaashaa qaamuuska caadiga ah ee Schema.org oo lagu kabay ereyo u gaar ah oo lagu sharraxay qaamuuska metadata ee web-kan [https://vojtamaur.cz/ns/]. Sidaas ayay ku ilaalisaa macnaha beeraha gaarka ah iyo xidhiidhka ka dhexeeya noocyada luqadda ee maqaallada.
 
Nuxurka web-ka waxaa sidoo kale gacanta loogu dhoofin karaa PDF, kaas oo ilaalinaya nuxurkiisa qaab ku habboon akhrinta, daabacaadda, iyo kaydinta muddada dheer ee ka madax bannaan web-ka laftiisa. Faahfaahinta ku saabsan habka loo abuurayo iyo qaab-dhismeedka dhoofinta ayaa lagu bixiyay dokumentiga farsamada ee kor ku xusan.
 
Sidoo kale, dhoofinta EPUB ayaa la abuuraa, oo loogu talagalay inta badan akhristayaasha elektaroonigga ah iyo software-ka kale ee la shaqeeya buugaagta elektaroonigga ah. Marka la barbar dhigo PDF, waxay isticmaashaa qulqulka qoraalka ee la qabsan karo waxayna matalaysaa matalaad kale oo ka mid ah nuxurka web-ka.
 
Matalaad gooni ah oo ka mid ah nuxurka ayaa sidoo kale samaysa dhoofinta SSTV laga bilaabo 10. Sebtembar 2026, taas oo si toos ah ugu beddesha qoraalka iyo nuxurka sawirka ee web-ka bogag loo habeeyay gudbinta sawirka caadiga ah ee Slow-Scan Television (SSTV) ee qaabka PD120 (640 × 496 px). Bogagga gaarka ah waxaa loo koodhay sidii gudbinta codka ee qaabka WAV waxaana dib loogu soo bandhigi karaa qalabka wax lagu qaato ee SSTV ee caadiga ah iyada oo aan loo baahnayn HTML-kii asalka ahaa ama browser-ka web-ka. Dhoofinta dhammaystiran waxay leedahay qiyaastii 21 GB iyo 61 saacadood oo cod ah.
 
Soo dejiso dhoofinta SSTV (Internet Archive) [https://archive.org/details/vojtamaur-cz-sstv-edition]
 
Marka laga reebo dhoofinta mashiinka lagu sameeyay, waxaa sidoo kale jira dhoofin hadal ah oo lix saacadood ah oo web-ka ah laga bilaabo 29. Agoosto 2026, taas oo aan si tartiib tartiib ah u dhex marayo, u akhrinayo, oo aan ugu faalloonayo ereyadayda nuxurkeeda. Diiwaangelintu, marka lagu daro qoraallada laftooda, waxay sidoo kale qabataa macnaha guud ee dheeraadka ah, xusuusta, sharraxaadaha, iyo macluumaadka aan si toos ah uga mid ahayn web-ka.
 
Ciyaar dhoofinta hadalka ee web-ka (YouTube) [https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xOdeVAuI-Wc]
 
LAKKAANKA KAYDINTA EE OFFLINE-KA
 
Qaybaha la xushay ee nuxurka web-ka ayaa sidoo kale ka jira warbaahin kaydinta jireed oo gooni ah, kuwaas oo inta badan si jireed uga go'an kaabayaasha shabakadda oo sidaas u samaynaya lakab kaydinta air-gapped. Kuwaas waxaa ka mid ah nuqullo analog ah oo la daabacay iyo warbaahin xogeed la qaadi karo. Warbaahinta gaarka ah way ku kala duwan yihiin habka akhrinta, ku-tiirsanaanta farsamada, maamulka, helitaanka, iyo cimriga la filayo. Qaar si toos ah ayaa loo akhrin karaa iyada oo aan loo baahnayn qalab elektaroonig ah, halka kuwa kalena ay u baahan yihiin qalab ku habboon, software, ama dhexdhexaadin hay'ad kaydinta.
 
Khad kasta oo ka mid ah diiwaanka soo socda wuxuu tilmaamayaa hal nuqul oo jireed oo gaar ah.

============================================================
Qashinka looxa nuqul-qaadista
 
 Abriil 2026 
 
 
 
Mashruucani wuxuu la shaqeeyaa waxa ku jira looxa nuqul-qaadista ee nidaamka Windows (clipboard [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schr%C3%A1nka_(software)]).
 Qoraal kasta, sawir, ama fayl kasta oo la nuqul-qaado, waxaa isla markiiba la geliyaa meel xaddidan.
 Waxyaabaha ku jira lama tafatiro mana la kala sooco. Waxyaabaha gaarka ah ayaa si aan kala sooc lahayn loogu meeleeyaa qaabka oo way is dul-saaran yihiin.
 Shiraacu wuxuu si tartiib tartiib ah u buuxsamayaa iyada oo aan suurtagal ahayn in dib loo noqdo ama la tafatiro.
 
Sawir kasta oo hoose waa natiijada hal maalin wuxuuna qabanayaa habsami-u-socodka shaqadayda kombiyuutarka.

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/clipboard-smetiste-obr-1.png] 
25. 3. 2026
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/clipboard-smetiste-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/clipboard-smetiste-obr-2.png] 
26. 3. 2026

Koodhka isha
 Koodhka Python ee isha mashruucan ayaa laga heli karaa halkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
import textwrap
from datetime import datetime
import tkinter as tk

import pyperclip
from PIL import Image, ImageTk, ImageGrab, ImageDraw, ImageFont

# =========================
# Nastavení
# =========================
CANVAS_SIZE = 700
BG_COLOR = "white"
POLL_MS = 500
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 320
TEXT_WRAP_CHARS = 38
TEXT_FONT = ("Arial", 14)
TEXT_COLOR = "black"
FILE_COLOR = "#1f4e79"

# =========================
# Tkinter okno
# =========================
root = tk.Tk()
root.title("Clipboard Canvas")
root.geometry(f"{CANVAS_SIZE}x{CANVAS_SIZE}")

canvas = tk.Canvas(root, width=CANVAS_SIZE, height=CANVAS_SIZE, bg=BG_COLOR, highlightthickness=0)
canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

# =========================
# PIL "skutečné" plátno pro export do PNG
# =========================
board = Image.new("RGB", (CANVAS_SIZE, CANVAS_SIZE), "white")
board_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(board)

try:
    pil_font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 20)
except Exception:
    pil_font = ImageFont.load_default()

# Tohle je důležité: držíme reference na VŠECHNY Tk obrázky,
# jinak Tkinter staré obrázky "uklidí" a zmizí.
tk_images = []

last_text = None
last_image_hash = None
last_file_list = None

# =========================
# Pomocné funkce
# =========================
def get_random_position(item_w, item_h):
    """Vrátí náhodnou pozici tak, aby se objekt vešel celý do plátna."""
    item_w = min(item_w, CANVAS_SIZE)
    item_h = min(item_h, CANVAS_SIZE)

    max_x = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_w)
    max_y = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_h)

    x = random.randint(0, max_x)
    y = random.randint(0, max_y)
    return x, y

def wrap_text_for_display(text, width=TEXT_WRAP_CHARS):
    lines = []
    for paragraph in text.splitlines() or [""]:
        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(paragraph, width=width) or [""]
        lines.extend(wrapped)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def measure_text_block(text):
    """Hrubý odhad velikosti textového bloku pro umístění."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    max_len = max((len(line) for line in lines), default=1)
    w = min(CANVAS_SIZE, 12 + max_len * 9)
    h = 12 + len(lines) * 24
    return w, h

def draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text, color=TEXT_COLOR, prefix=None):
    display_text = f"{prefix}{text}" if prefix else text
    wrapped = wrap_text_for_display(display_text)
    w, h = measure_text_block(wrapped)
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    canvas.create_text(
        x, y,
        anchor="nw",
        text=wrapped,
        fill=color,
        font=TEXT_FONT,
        width=w
    )

    # PIL board
    board_draw.multiline_text((x, y), wrapped, fill=color, font=pil_font, spacing=6)

def draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(img):
    # zmenšíme kopii, originál neni potřeba
    img = img.convert("RGB")
    img.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_SIZE, MAX_IMAGE_SIZE))

    w, h = img.size
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    tk_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
    tk_images.append(tk_img)
    canvas.create_image(x, y, anchor="nw", image=tk_img)

    # PIL board
    board.paste(img, (x, y))

def hash_pil_image(img):
    # Rychlejší než hash(img.tobytes()) na obrovských datech:
    tiny = img.copy()
    tiny.thumbnail((64, 64))
    return hash(tiny.tobytes())

def save_canvas_png(event=None):
    filename = f"clipboard_canvas_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
    board.save(filename, "PNG")
    print(f"Uloženo: {os.path.abspath(filename)}")
    
# =========================
# Clipboard monitoring
# =========================
def check_clipboard():
    global last_text, last_image_hash, last_file_list

    # 1) Zkusit obrázek / seznam souborů
    try:
        clip = ImageGrab.grabclipboard()

        # Obrázek
        if isinstance(clip, Image.Image):
            img_hash = hash_pil_image(clip)
            if img_hash != last_image_hash:
                last_image_hash = img_hash
                draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(clip)

        # Soubory
        elif isinstance(clip, list):
            normalized = tuple(map(str, clip))
            if normalized != last_file_list:
                last_file_list = normalized
                for path in normalized:
                    draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(path, color=FILE_COLOR, prefix="[FILE] ")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2) Zkusit text
    try:
        text = pyperclip.paste()
        if isinstance(text, str):
            text = text.strip()
            if text and text != last_text:
                last_text = text
                draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text)
    except Exception:
        pass

    root.after(POLL_MS, check_clipboard)

# =========================
# Klávesy
# =========================
root.bind("<KeyPress-s>", save_canvas_png)
root.bind("<KeyPress-S>", save_canvas_png)
root.focus_force()

# start
check_clipboard()
root.mainloop()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Hay'ado ku-meel-gaar ah
SLUG: docasne-entity
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/docasne-entity/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-01-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/docasne-entity.mdx
BUILT_HTML: docasne-entity/index.html
============================================================
Hay'ado ku-meel-gaar ah
 
 Janaayo 2026 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/TS4kEbk3SGs]
 
 
Mashruucani waa matalaad farshaxan oo ku saabsan sida ay u dhalan karaan maskaxaha Boltzmann [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boltzmann_brain] iyo hay'ado kale oo ku-meel-gaar ah, kuwaas oo si kedis ah uga soo baxa entropy iyada oo loo marayo qaabeynta aan kala sooca lahayn ee qaybaha, ka dibna ku dhex baaba'a.
 
Dunida dhabta ah ee fiisigiska, kormeerka ifafaale noocan oo kale ah wuxuu u baahan lahaa waqti aan la qiyaasi karin oo dheer.
 
Taasi waa sababta aan si firfircoon u beddelay xawaaraha qaybaha gaarka ah si ay suurtogal u noqoto in la muujiyo dhalashada iyo dhimashada boqol hay'adood waqti macquul ah. Ma aanan faragelin dariiqyada qaybaha haba yaraatee.
 
Hay'aduhu waxay u dhasheen si aan u doortay boqol eray oo aan kala sooc lahayn oo ka mid ah qaamuuska, waxaan u qoondeeyay sawir mid kasta oo matalaya, ka dibna waxaan u oggolaaday inuu dib uga soo baxo goobta entropy ee qaybaha.
 
Waa isku day lagu muujinayo sida macnaha, qaabka, ama fikraddu ay uga soo bixi karaan qaska muddo gaaban, iyada oo aan lahayn wax sheegasho ah oo ku saabsan jiritaan joogto ah.

TITLE: Genome-ka ku jira wareegga Theodore
SLUG: genom-theodorove-spirale

SECTION: volna-tvorba

SOURCE: src/content/posts/genom-theodorove-spirale.mdx
BUILT_HTML: genom-theodorove-spirale/index.html
============================================================
Genome-ka ku jira wareegga Theodore
 
 Janaayo 2026 
 
 
 
Muuqaalkan ayaa u beddelaya macluumaadka hidde-sidaha ee toosan (DNA/RNA) mid joogto ah 2D wareegga Theodore [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spiral_of_Theodorus].
 
Dhibic kasta oo ku taal wareegga waxay u dhigantaa hal saldhig oo ku jira nidaamka ay dhab ahaan ugu jirto isku-xigxiga. Bilowga isku-xigxiga wuxuu ku yaallaa bartamaha wareegga. Midabka dhibicdu wuxuu matalaa nooca saldhigga.
 
Wareeggani wuxuu suurtogelinayaa in la muujiyo xog 1D dhaadheer oo ku yaal aag 2D yar, iyada oo aan la goyn socodka macluumaadka. Ma jiraan wax kala goyn ah oo macmal ah, sida ku noqoshada bilowga sadarka, kuwaas oo caadi ka ah matalaadaha qoraalka.
 
Tusaale ahaan, waxaan muuqaal ka dhigay genome-yada saddex noole oo leh dherer kala duwan. Dhammaantood waxay leeyihiin genome toosan, kaas oo ah kan ugu habboon qaabkan muuqaalaynta.
 
HIV-1: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/170878295]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png] 
 
Fayraska Ebola: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/10313991?utm_]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png] 
 
SaRS-CoV-2: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/OM065388.1]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png] 
 
Koodhka isha
 Koodhka Python ee isha mashruucan ayaa laga heli karaa halkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

# =========================
# KONFIG
# =========================

SEQ_TXT_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.txt"          # cesta k souboru (FASTA nebo prostý text)
OUT_PNG_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.png"          # kam uložit PNG výstup

WIDTH_PX  = 4000
HEIGHT_PX = 4000

SHOW_LEGEND = False  # defaultně žádný text
LEGEND_TEXT = "genom na Theodorově spirále"

# RGBK (A,C,G,T/U -> R,G,B,K)
COLORS = {
    "A": (1.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Red
    "C": (0.0, 1.0, 0.0),  # Green
    "G": (0.0, 0.0, 1.0),  # Blue
    "T": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Black (Key)
    "U": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # RNA U taky do K
}

# Vzhled
DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE = False
LINE_WIDTH = 0.12
LINE_ALPHA = 0.25

POINT_SIZE = 90.0
POINT_ALPHA = 1

CROP_PADDING_RATIO = 0.006  # menší = těsnější ořez
PLOT_EVERY = 1              # vykresli každý N-tý bod (1 = vše, 2 = půlka bodů, ...)

BACKGROUND = "white"        # nebo "black"
TRANSPARENT_BG = False      # true = průhledné PNG

# =========================
# IMPLEMENTACE
# =========================

def read_sequence(path: str) -> str:
    """Načte sekvenci a nechá jen A/C/G/T/U."""
    txt = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").upper()
    seq = "".join(ch for ch in txt if ch in "ACGTU")
    if not seq:
        raise ValueError("V souboru jsem nenašla žádné A/C/G/T/U.")
    return seq

def theodorus_vertices(n: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Souřadnice vrcholů Theodorovy spirály pro indexy 0..n (0 = origin)."""
    theta = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    if n >= 2:
        theta[2:] = np.cumsum(np.arctan(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, n))))
    r = np.sqrt(np.arange(0, n + 1, dtype=float))
    x = r * np.cos(theta)
    y = r * np.sin(theta)
    return x, y

def main():
    seq = read_sequence(SEQ_TXT_PATH)
    if PLOT_EVERY > 1:
        seq = seq[::PLOT_EVERY]

    n = len(seq)
    x, y = theodorus_vertices(n)

    pos = np.arange(1, n + 1)  # vrchol i mapuje seq[i-1]
    seq_bytes = np.frombuffer(seq.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8)
    counts = Counter(seq)

    # Figsize = pixely / dpi; dpi si zvolíme jen jako interní převod.
    dpi_internal = 100
    figsize = (WIDTH_PX / dpi_internal, HEIGHT_PX / dpi_internal)

    fig = plt.figure(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi_internal, facecolor=BACKGROUND)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1])  # žádné okraje, čistý full-bleed
    ax.set_facecolor(BACKGROUND)

    if DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE:
        ax.plot(
            x[1:], y[1:],
            linewidth=LINE_WIDTH,
            color=(0, 0, 0),
            alpha=LINE_ALPHA
        )

    for base in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"]:
        mask = (seq_bytes == ord(base))
        if not np.any(mask):
            continue
        idxs = pos[mask]
        ax.scatter(
            x[idxs], y[idxs],
            s=POINT_SIZE,
            c=[COLORS[base]],
            alpha=POINT_ALPHA,
            linewidths=0
        )

    # Tight crop + vycentrování na formát
    xmin, xmax = x[1:].min(), x[1:].max()
    ymin, ymax = y[1:].min(), y[1:].max()
    padx = (xmax - xmin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    pady = (ymax - ymin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    ax.set_xlim(xmin - padx, xmax + padx)
    ax.set_ylim(ymin - pady, ymax + pady)

    ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
    ax.axis("off")

    if SHOW_LEGEND:
        title_counts = ", ".join(f"{b}:{counts.get(b, 0)}" for b in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"])
        ax.text(
            0.5, 0.985,
            f"{LEGEND_TEXT}\nN={n:,}, krok={PLOT_EVERY} | {title_counts}",
            ha="center", va="top",
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            fontsize=14,
            color="black" if BACKGROUND == "white" else "white"
        )

    fig.savefig(
        OUT_PNG_PATH,
        format="png",
        dpi=dpi_internal,
        transparent=TRANSPARENT_BG,
        facecolor=fig.get_facecolor()
    )
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Uloženo: {OUT_PNG_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

[/CODE BLOCK]

============================================================
TITLE: Ka-luuqad
SLUG: anti-jazyk
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/anti-jazyk/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2025-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/anti-jazyk.mdx
BUILT_HTML: anti-jazyk/index.html
============================================================
Ka-luuqad
 
 Diseembar 2025 
 
 
 
Qaababka luuqadda ee caadiga ah waxay doortaan calaamadda xigta iyagoo dooranaya mid ka mid ah soo-jeedimaha ugu macquulsan, kuwaas oo sida ay qiyaastooda tirakoobku tilmaamayso ugu habboon macnaha guud. Tan waxaan ugu yeeri doonnaa muunad-qaadista caadiga ah.
 Mashruucani wuxuu sameeyaa wax ka duwan taas.
 Halkii laga dooran lahaa calaamadaha ugu macquulsan, waxaan ku qasbaynaa qaabka inuu gaaro kuwa ugu yar ee macquulka ah, kuwaas oo ah kuwa uu ku dhowaad marnaba isticmaali lahayn xaalad caadi ah.
 Natiijadu waa anti-jazyk – qoraal ka kooban qaybo ka mid ah ereyada qaabka oo ah kuwo xaddidan, la ciqaabay, oo inta badan ka fog.
 Tani waxay abuurtaa qaab-dhismeedyo aan ahayn calaamado aan kala sooc lahayn, laakiin sidoo kale aan ahayn weedho isku xiran.
 
Sida ay u shaqeyso
 Qaabka waxaa la siiyaa wax-soo-gelin gaaban (tusaale, "This is ").
 Wuxuu soo saaraa qaybinta suurtagalnimada ee dhammaan calaamadaha kale ee suurtogalka ah.
 Halkii laga dooran lahaa qaybta sare ee qaybinta (calaamadaha ugu macquulsan), habka oo dhan waa la rogayaa waxaana laga dooranayaa gunta hoose (calaamadaha ugu yar ee macquulka ah).
 Tani waxay daaha ka qaadaysaa "lakabyada hoose" ee qaabka: ereyo dhif ah, jajab luuqadeed, iyo waxyaabo kale oo qaabku uu caadi ahaan caburiyo.
 
Koodhka
 Koodhka waxaa lagu socodsiin karaa Google Colab:
 https://colab.research.google.com/drive/18y4hUsNv-aEZHtXVp67ygHGGpMBkByxD?usp=sharing
 
Colab halkan wuxuu u adeegaa inta badan sidii jawi socodsiin. Ma aha nuqulka kaydka ah ee ugu muhiimsan ee koodhka.
 Taasi waa sababta habka la midka ah loogu kaydiyay hoos si toos ah maqaalka dhexdiisa.
 
Koodhka waxaa loo qaybiyay saddex qaybood oo kala duwan sida ku jirta buug-yaraha. Midka koowaad wuxuu rartaa qaabka, kan labaad wuxuu soo saaraa qoraalka, kan saddexaadna wuxuu kaydiyaa wax-soo-saarka ugu dambeeya.
 
1. Rarista qaabka iyo tokenizer-ka
 Qaybtan waxaa ku filan in la socodsiiyo hal mar. Waxay rartaa qaabka GPT-2 iyo tokenizer-ka.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import torch
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("gpt2")  # also works: gpt2-medium, gpt2-large, gpt2-xl, EleutherAI/gpt-neo-1.3B
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("gpt2")
model.eval()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
2. Soo saarista ka-luuqadda
 Qaybtan, waxaad bedeli kartaa qoraalka wax-soo-gelinta, tirada calaamadaha cusub, iyo qiimaha bottom_k.
 Marka uu bottom_k yaraado, ayaa qaabka si adag loogu riixayaa gunta hoose ee qaybinta suurtagalnimada.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
prompt = "This is "
input_ids = tokenizer.encode(prompt, return_tensors="pt")

max_new_tokens = 50
bottom_k = 5

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids)
        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1, :]

        probs = torch.softmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1)

        bottom_probs, bottom_indices = torch.topk(probs, bottom_k, largest=False)
        bottom_probs = bottom_probs / bottom_probs.sum()

        chosen_index = torch.multinomial(bottom_probs, num_samples=1)
        next_token_id = bottom_indices[chosen_index]

        input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids, next_token_id.unsqueeze(0)], dim=-1)

generated_text = tokenizer.decode(input_ids.squeeze(), skip_special_tokens=True)
print(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
3. Kaydinta wax-soo-saarka ugu dambeeya
 Qaybtan waxay kaydinaysaa qoraalkii ugu dambeeyay ee la soo saaray faylka output.txt.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
with open("output.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
Miisaanka qaabka GPT-2 kuma jiro bogga. Marka la bilaabayo, waxaa laga soo dejisanayaa il dibadda ah.
 Halkan waxaa inta badan lagu kaydiyay habka: tilmaamo yar oo ku saabsan sida loo qasbo qaabka inuu soo saaro qashinka tirakoobka halkii uu ka isticmaali lahaa luuqad caadi ah.
 
Tusaalaha wax-soo-saarka
 Halkan waxaan u isticmaalay qaabka GPT-2 waxaana u daayay inuu dhammaystiro qoraalka "This is ":

Falanqaynta tusaalaha wax-soo-saarka
 Ka-luuqaddu ma aha buuq aan kala sooc lahayn. Taxane jajab ah ayaa xusuusinaya raadadka qaybaha xogta gaarka ah ee qaabku u badan tahay inuu arkay, laakiin kuwaas oo muunad-qaadista caadiga ah aysan ku dhowaad marnaba soo wicin, sababtoo ah waa kuwo aan faa'iido u lahayn dhanka tirakoobka. Ka-luuqaddu, dhanka kale, way ka faa'iidaysataa.
 Qaybo ka mid ah qoraalka uu qaabku soo saaro waxay u muuqdaan kuwo ka yimid xogta tababarka. Mararka qaarkood waxaa la arki karaa tilmaamo ku saabsan xogta laga soo ururiyay dukaamada internetka ee kala duwan ("BuyableInstoreAndOnline", "SpecialOrderable", "ItemThumbnailImage"), mawduucyo laga yaabo inay xasaasi yihiin ama bulsho ahaan culus ("sexual", "Muslims", "Hispanic"), ereyo cilmiyeed ama qaybo ka mid ah ("isomer", "antidepress", "[m]icrobial", "[p]uncture", "intolerance"), tixraacyo dhaqameed ("Monroe", "Phelps", "Rockefeller", "Nitrome"), waxyaabo diini ah-khuraafaad ah ama khayaali ah ("Archdemon", "devils"), meelo ("Moroc", "Afric"), qaybo ka mid ah interface-ka isticmaalaha ee mareegaha ("cloneembedreportprint", "IMAGES"), iwm.
 Waa lagama maarmaan in lagu daro xaddidaadda falanqayntan: maskaxdu waxay u janjeertaa inay soo saarto qaabab la yaqaan (summadaha, magacyada, fikradaha) oo ay iska indho-tirto inta kale. Intaa waxaa dheer, qaar ka mid ah ereyada ayaa leh macno laba-geesood ah ("intolerance" waxay noqon kartaa mid caafimaad iyo mid bulsho labadaba), markaa tarjumaaddoodu ma aha mid cad.

Dirxi ε
 
 Sebtember 2025 
 
 
 
Qeexid:
 Aasaasku waa shabakad neerfaha oo il-furan (open-source) ee dirxiga C. elegans. Miisaan kasta wᵢ′ waa la beddelay:
 wᵢ′ = wᵢ + εᵢ
 halkaas oo εᵢ ay tahay doorsoome madax-bannaan oo aan kala sooc lahayn oo leh qaybin siman oo ku jirta kala duwanaanshaha [−10⁻⁴,10⁻⁴].
 
Dhiirrigelin:
 Ku darista tiro yar oo aan la xisaabin karin εᵢ uma adeegto hagaajinta qaabka. Waxay u adeegtaa oo keliya in la helo dirxi la qeexay oo aan suurtagal ahayn in si buuxda loo xisaabiyo. Taasi waa ujeeddadiisa keliya.

============================================================
DullGPT
 
 Luulyo 2024 
 
 
 
DullGPT waa tijaabo fikir ah oo muujinaysa suurtagalnimada abuurista nidaam sirdoon macmal ah iyadoo la adeegsanayo hab aad u fudud (laakiin aad u waxtar yar). Mashruucani wuxuu ka kooban yahay barnaamij soo saara dhammaan waxyaabaha suurtagalka ah ee la gelin karo, u gudbiya qaabka ChatGPT, oo kaydiya jawaabaha la helay. Sidaas ayuu si tartiib tartiib ah u dhisayaa xog-ururin (database) ka kooban waxyaabaha la geliyay iyo jawaabaha, taas oo abuuraysa dhalanteed ah nidaam caqli badan.
 
DullGPT waxay leedahay laba hab: "barasho" iyo "isticmaalka". Habka barashada, barnaamijku wuxuu soo saaraa waxyaabaha la geliyo wuxuuna ururiyaa jawaabaha, halka habka isticmaalka uu u oggolaanayo isticmaalaha inuu geliyo waxyaabo oo uu ka helo jawaabo xog-ururinta.
 
Asal ahaan, waa kiis aad u daran oo ah habka xoogga saafi ah (brute-force) ee lagu jilayo sirdoonka. Halkii uu isku dayi lahaa inuu fahmo macnaha waxyaabaha la geliyay oo uu u soo saari lahaa jawaabo ku salaysan qaab barasho, wuxuu si fudud u ururiyaa oo u kaydiyaa tiro aad u badan oo lammaane ah oo ka kooban waxyaabaha la geliyay iyo jawaabaha. Marka laga eego dhinaca dabeecadda, waa inaan lagu kala saarin ChatGPT, haddii uu haysto xog ku filan. Tani waxay dhalinaysaa su'aal ah in fahamka dhabta ah iyo sirdoonku ay gabi ahaanba lagama maarmaan u yihiin abuurista isgaarsiin caqli badan. Si kale haddii loo dhigo: DullGPT waxay abuurtaa jawaabo aan lagu kala saari karin kuwa uu soo saaray nidaam dhab ah oo caqli badan, laakiin waxay ka maqan yihiin nooc kasta oo khibrad dhab ah ama is-milicsi ah.
 
DullGPT, dabcan, marka la barbar dhigo ChatGPT, waxay leedahay xaddidaadyo badan, kuwaas oo ay ka mid yihiin xawaaraha "tababarka" iyo ku tiirsanaanta moodooyinka kale ee waaweyn ee luqadda, iyada oo aan la tixgelin taariikhda wada hadalka, iyo iyada oo aan la tixgelin dhammaan calaamadaha la geliyay (tusaale ahaan, xarfaha Shiinaha, iwm.). Si ka duwan moodooyinka waaweyn ee luqadda, waxay had iyo jeer siin doontaa jawaab isku mid ah isla waxyaabaha la geliyay. Maadaama ay tahay oo kaliya tijaabo fikir ah oo aan lahayn isticmaalka dhabta ah, macno ma samaynayso in la isku dayo in la saxo cilladahan.
 
Si kastaba ha ahaatee, waxaa xusid mudan inaanan isku dayin inaan ku difaaco doodda qolka Shiinaha (Chinese room argument), taas oo su'aal ka keenaysa suurtagalnimada in la gaaro sirdoon dhab ah ama faham kaliya iyada oo lagu salaynayo wax ka qabashada calaamadaha iyada oo aan lahayn faham dhab ah. Waxaan kaliya ku doodayaa in ugu yaraan hal nidaam oo caqli badan oo noocaas ah la abuuri karo, xitaa haddii ay tahay hab aan waxtar lahayn oo doqonnimo ah. Laakiin uma malaynayo in nidaam kastaa uu qasab ku yahay inuu noqdo mid madhan gudaha.
 
Halkan waxaa ah koodhka isha ee barnaamijkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import openai
import sqlite3
import string
import itertools
import sys
import os
import pickle

# Nastavení API klíče
OPENAI_API_KEY = "SEM VLOŽ SVŮJ OPENAI API KEY"
openai.api_key = OPENAI_API_KEY

# Cesta k databázi a log souboru
DATABASE_PATH = "dullgpt_db.sqlite"
LOG_FILE = "log.txt"
LAST_INPUT_FILE = "last_input.pkl"

# Funkce pro komunikaci s OpenAI API
def query_chatgpt(prompt):
    response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
        model="gpt-3.5-turbo",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a helpful assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ]
    )
    return response['choices'][0]['message']['content'].strip()

# Funkce pro generování vstupů
def generate_inputs(length):
    characters = string.ascii_letters + string.digits + string.punctuation + " "
    return [''.join(i) for i in itertools.product(characters, repeat=length)]

# Funkce pro vytvoření databáze
def create_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS responses (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            input TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT NOT NULL
        )
    ''')
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro ukládání odpovědí do databáze
def save_response(input_text, output_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO responses (input, output) VALUES (?, ?)
    ''', (input_text, output_text))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro získání odpovědi z databáze
def get_response(input_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        SELECT output FROM responses WHERE input = ?
    ''', (input_text,))
    result = c.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return result[0] if result else None

# Funkce pro uložení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def save_last_input(input_text):
    with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'wb') as f:
        pickle.dump(input_text, f)

# Funkce pro načtení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def load_last_input():
    if os.path.exists(LAST_INPUT_FILE):
        with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            return pickle.load(f)
    return None

# Funkce pro logování párů vstup-výstup
def log_response(input_text, output_text):
    with open(LOG_FILE, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}\n")

# Režim "učení"
def learn_mode():
    create_db()
    last_input = load_last_input()
    start_length = 1 if not last_input else len(last_input)
    start_index = 0

    if last_input:
        print(f"Loaded last input: '{last_input}'")
        all_inputs = generate_inputs(len(last_input))
        if last_input in all_inputs:
            start_index = all_inputs.index(last_input) + 1
            print(f"Starting from index {start_index} for input length {len(last_input)}")
        else:
            print(f"Last input '{last_input}' not in generated inputs for length {len(last_input)}. Starting from beginning.")
            start_length = len(last_input) + 1
            last_input = None  # Reset last_input to start from the beginning

    while True:  # Loop to continue processing inputs of increasing length
        inputs = generate_inputs(start_length)
        if last_input and start_length == len(last_input):
            start_index = inputs.index(last_input) + 1
        else:
            start_index = 0
        for input_text in inputs[start_index:]:
            output_text = query_chatgpt(input_text)
            save_response(input_text, output_text)
            log_response(input_text, output_text)
            print(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}")
            save_last_input(input_text)
        last_input = None
        start_length += 1

# Režim "užívání"
def use_mode():
    create_db()
    while True:
        user_input = input("Enter your input: ")
        output_text = get_response(user_input)
        if output_text:
            print(f"Output: {output_text}")
        else:
            print("No response found for this input.")

# Hlavní část programu
if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2 or sys.argv[1] not in ("learn", "use"):
        print("Usage: python dullgpt.py [learn|use]")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    if sys.argv[1] == "learn":
        learn_mode()
    else:
        use_mode()

Xuduuda asturnaanta
 
 Febraayo 2024 
 
 
 
Waxaan sameeyay barnaamij fudud oo lagu qeexi karo hal ama in ka badan oo hagayaal ah (directories). Waxa uu baaraa dhammaantood oo ay ku jiraan hoosaadyadooda, markii uu la kulmo sawir kasta (sawir halkan waxaan uga jeedaa fayl ku dhammaada kordhinta .jpg, .jpeg, .png, .tif, .tiff), waxa uu jidkiisa ku daraa ururinta dhammaan sawirrada. Marka baadhitaanku dhammaado, waxa uu ka xushaa hal sawir oo keliya oo uu soo bandhigaa.
 
Ka dib waxaan kombiyuutarkayga ku xidhay dhammaan hard-disk-yadii kaydka ahaa ee sawirrada aan nolosheyda oo dhan ururiyay (hadda waxa ku jira in ka badan 91 000 sawirro). Dhammaan hard-disk-yada waxaan u dejiyay sidii hagayaal aasaasi ah oo barnaamijku ka xusho sawirrada. Sababo la xidhiidha wax ku oolnimada, waxaa lagama maarmaan ahayd in la mamnuuco xulashada qaar ka mid ah hagayaasha, sababtoo ah qaar ka mid ah waxay ka koobnaayeen xogta barnaamijyada oo keliya, sida astaamahooda. Markii la riixo furaha ENTER, barnaamijku wuxuu doortay sawir aan kala sooc lahayn oo nolosheyda ka mid ah. Markii mar kale la riixo ENTER, barnaamijku wuxuu doortay sawir kale, iwm...
 
Sawirrada aan kala sooca lahayn ee la doortay, waxaan ka sameeyay dhowr taxane oo isku xidhan. Taxane kasta wuxuu ku bilaabmaa xulasho aan kala sooc lahayn oo sawir ah wuxuuna sii wadaa xulashada sawirro kale oo aan kala sooc lahayn ilaa uu ka helo mid aanan rabin inaan daabaco. Sawirkan ugu dambeeya ayaa taxanaha lagu soo bandhigay isagoo qallafsan (blurred). Waxaan xiisaynayay inaan ogaado sawirrada keena in taxanuhu dhammaado iyo sababta. Xaaladaha badankood, si aan ula yaabay, waxaa soo afjaray walaaca ku saabsan asturnaanta dadka kale. Xaaladaha kale, waxaa soo afjaray sawirrada dukumentiyada shaqada, guryaha dadka kale iyo gurigayga, iwm.
 
Mashruucan, waxaan ku ogaaday sida ay u ceeryaamo badan yihiin xuduudaha asturnaantaydu, haddii ay jiraanba. Waqti cayiman, waa inaan go'aansadaa inaan sawirka daabaco iyo in kale. Laakiin doorashadan inta badan way adkayd oo aan caddayn.

Koodka isha
 Koodka Python ee mashruucan ayaa laga heli karaa halkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

def open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths):
    # Převod cest na absolutní cesty
    folder_paths = [Path(folder).resolve() for folder in folder_paths]
    excluded_paths = [Path(excluded).resolve() for excluded in excluded_paths]

    # Seznam podporovaných přípon souborů
    supported_extensions = ['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', 'tif', 'tiff']

    # Seznam pro ukládání cest k obrázkům
    images = []

    # Prochází všechny zadané adresáře
    for folder_path in folder_paths:
        # Prochází soubory v adresáři a jeho podsložkách
        for path in folder_path.rglob('*'):
            # Přidává cesty k obrázkům, pokud mají podporovanou příponu
            if path.suffix.lower() in supported_extensions:
                images.append(path)

    while True:
        if not images:
            print("V zadaných adresářích nebyly nalezeny žádné obrázky.")
            return

        # Náhodný výběr souboru
        image_path = random.choice(images)

        # Kontroluje, zda vybraná cesta neleží v některém z vyloučených adresářů
        if any(image_path.is_relative_to(excluded) for excluded in excluded_paths):
            continue  # Pokud je cesta vyloučená, vybere jiný obrázek

        print(f"Otevírám obrázek: {image_path}")
        os.startfile(image_path)
        return

# Seznam cest k adresářům
folder_paths = [
    r"D:\\02_záloha dat",
    r"G:",
    r"K:"
    #r"C:"
]

# Seznam cest k vyloučení
excluded_paths = [
    r"K:\\WEB",
]

while True:  # Nekonečná smyčka
    open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths)
    input("Stiskni ENTER pro opětovné spuštění nebo Ctrl+C pro ukončení programu...")

[/CODE BLOCK]

Shaqooyinka hirarka
 
 Janaayo 2024 
 
 
 
Mashruucani waa muujinta sida loo simi karo faafitaanka iyo faragelinta hirarka iyo isdhexgalkooda deegaanka, iyadoo la adeegsanayo moodallo xisaabeed iyo jilaid kombuyuutar. Isticmaalka qiyamka aan kala sooca lahayn ee booska ilaha hirarka, inta jeer ee ay dhacayaan iyo baaxaddooda, oo ay weheliso hirgelinta baaxadda oo hoos u dhacaysa marka ay fogaato masaafada ilaha iyo ka tarjumidda hirarka ee geesaha labajibbaaranaha iyo faragelintooda midba midka kale, waxay abuurtaa qaabab muuqaal ahaan xiiso leh oo muujinaya kakanaanta isdhexgalka hirarka ee meel xaddidan. Natiijooyinka waxaa lagu muujiyey iyadoo la adeegsanayo miisaanka midabka madow iyo caddaanka, kaas oo matalaya xoojinta hirarka ee dhibcaha kala duwan ee booska labajibbaaran.

Koodhka isha
 Koodhka isha ee Python ee mashruucan ayaa laga heli karaa halkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import re

# Počet obrázků k vytvoření
num_images = 1

# Nastavení rozsahů pro náhodné hodnoty
frequency_range = (1, 50)  # Rozsah frekvencí vln
amplitude_range = (0.1, 1)  # Rozsah amplitud vln

# Počet zdrojů
number_of_sources = 3

# Velikost prostoru pro simulaci a rozlišení mřížky
space_size = 5
grid_resolution = 1000

for i in range(num_images):
    # Inicializace pole pro simulaci
    Z = np.zeros((grid_resolution, grid_resolution))

    # Vytvoření mřížky pro simulaci
    x = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    y = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(x, y)

    # Generování náhodných zdrojů
    for _ in range(number_of_sources):
        source_position = np.random.uniform(-space_size, space_size, 2)
        frequency = np.random.uniform(*frequency_range)
        amplitude = np.random.uniform(*amplitude_range)

        # Výpočet vzdálenosti od zdroje a přidání vlny do simulace
        R = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
        Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R) / (R + 1)  # Přidání útlumu amplitudy

        # Odrážení vln od stěn
        for wall in [-space_size, space_size]:
            R_vertical = np.sqrt((X - 2*wall + source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
            R_horizontal = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - 2*wall + source_position[1])**2)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_vertical) / (R_vertical + 1)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_horizontal) / (R_horizontal + 1)

    # Vykreslení výsledku
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 10))
    plt.imshow(Z, cmap='gray', extent=[-space_size, space_size, -space_size, space_size])
    plt.axis('off')
    
    # Před uložením obrázku použijte tight_layout pro optimalizaci rozložení
    #plt.tight_layout()

    # Uložení obrázku
    pattern = r"wave_interference_pattern_(\d+).png"
    existing_files = [f for f in os.listdir('.') if re.match(pattern, f)]
    if existing_files:
        numbers = [int(re.search(pattern, f).group(1)) for f in existing_files]
        max_number = max(numbers)
    else:
        max_number = 0
    file_name = f"wave_interference_pattern_{max_number + 1:05d}.png"
    plt.savefig(file_name, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0, transparent=False, dpi=(grid_resolution*1.3)//10)
    plt.close()  # Zavření obrázku po uložení

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Qaab-dhismeedka Recamán
SLUG: vystavy-recamanova-struktura
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/vystavy-recamanova-struktura/
LANGUAGE: so

Jidka dulinka
 
 Agoosto 2023 
 
 
 
Waxaa laga yaabaa inay u muuqato in la macaamilka maalinlaha ah ee jiirka kombiyuutarka uu yahay hawl caadi ah oo aan wax gaar ah lahayn. Laakiin ka warran haddii ay suurtogal tahay in dhaqdhaqaaqyadan caadiga ah loo beddelo wax ka badan, wax abuuraya matalaad muuqaal ah oo gaar ah oo ku saabsan shaqada kombiyuutarka? Waad ku mahadsan tahay koodhka qabta booska dulinka jiirka iyo midabka barta hoosteeda, fikraddan waxay noqon kartaa xaqiiqo.
 
Sidee ayay u shaqaysaa?
 Koodhkani wuxuu la socdaa halka uu dulinka jiirku ku yaallo shaashadda iyo midabka uu leeyahay barta hoosteeda. Macluumaadkan ayaa markaa lagu wareejiyaa wakhtiga dhabta ah shiraac farsamo oo leh xallin u dhigma xallinta shaashadda. Mar kasta oo jiirka la dhaqaajiyo, koodhku wuxuu sawiraa dhibic leh midabkii ka hooseeyay dulinka meeshii uu dulinku ku sugnaa. Tani waxay ka dhigan tahay in marka la shaqaynayo oo shaashadda la dhaqaajinayo, dariiqyo midab leh ay ka samaysmaan shiraaca. Dariiqyadan ayaa ah kuwo gaar ah oo matalaya shaqada iyo isdhexgalka isticmaalaha ee kombiyuutarka.
 
Maxaa dhacaya muddo dheer ka dib?
 Haddii habkani uu socdo muddo ku filan, shiraacu wuxuu bilaabaa inuu buuxsamo oo dariiqyadu waxay noqdaan kuwo aan si fudud loo dareemi karin. Halkii ay ka ahaan lahaayeen, inta badan shiraacu wuxuu bilaabaa inuu u ekaado buuq aan kala sooc lahayn. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, waxaa jira meelo gaar ah oo shaashadda ka mid ah oo aan wax badan iska beddelin waqti ka dib. Tani waxay noqon kartaa, tusaale ahaan, bar-hawleedka hoose ee Windows, halkaas oo ay ku yaalliin astaamaha software-ka. Meelahan shiraaca ku yaal waxay ahaanayaan kuwo aan isbeddelin.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png] 
 
Gabagabo
 Koodhkani wuxuu matalaa isku-dhafka tignoolajiyada iyo farshaxanka, kaas oo u oggolaanaya in la beddelo isdhexgalka maalinlaha ah ee kombiyuutarka loona beddelo wax muuqaal ahaan soo jiidasho leh. In kasta oo ay u muuqan karto marka hore in shaqada kombiyuutarku ay tahay mid caadi ah oo aan kala go' lahayn, mashruucani wuxuu muujinayaa in xitaa hawl noocaas ah ay yeelan karto quruxdeeda iyo laxankeeda.
 
Koodhka isha
 Koodhka Python ee mashruucan ayaa laga heli karaa halkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
 import pygame
import pyautogui
import sys
import time

# Inicializace Pygame
pygame.init()

# Získání rozměrů obrazovky
screen_info = pygame.display.Info()
screen_width = screen_info.current_w
screen_height = screen_info.current_h

# Vytvoření okna o rozměrech shodných s rozměry obrazovky
screen = pygame.display.set_mode((screen_width, screen_height))

# Hlavní smyčka programu
running = True
start_time = time.time()
while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            running = False
        elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
            if event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:  # Když stisknete klávesu ESC, okno se zavře
                running = False

    # Získání souřadnic kurzoru pomocí PyAutoGUI
    x, y = pyautogui.position()

    # Získání barvy pixelu pod kurzorem
    if 0 <= x < screen_width and 0 <= y < screen_height:  # Zkontrolujte, zda jsou souřadnice v rámci obrazovky
        try:
            r, g, b = pyautogui.pixel(x, y)
            # Nabarvení pixelu na plátně touto barvou
            screen.set_at((x, y), (r, g, b))
        except:
            pass

    # Aktualizace zobrazení
    pygame.display.flip()

    # Uložení záložního obrázku každé 3 minuty
    if time.time() - start_time >= 180:  # 180 sekund = 3 minuty
        pygame.image.save(screen, "output_backup.png")
        start_time = time.time()

# Uložení obrázku
pygame.image.save(screen, "output.png")

# Ukončení Pygame
pygame.quit()
sys.exit()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Diiwaangelinta dhirta

============================================================
Diiwaangelinta dhirta
 
 Juun 2023 
 
 
 
Hordhac
 Maqaalkan, waxaan ku eegaynaa mashruuc isku xiraya dunida dhirta, elektaroonigga, barnaamij-samaynta, iyo farshaxanka muuqaalka. Mashruucu wuxuu isticmaalaa Arduino iyo Python si loo abuuro muuqaallo ka tarjumaya waayo-aragnimada nolosha dhirta.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png | jaantuska xiriirinta arduino]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png] 
 
Sidee ayay u shaqaysaa?
 Arduino-ga waxaa lagu qalabeeyay saddex dareeme – heerkulbeeg, sawir-qaade (photoresistor), iyo qoyaanka cabbira. Dareemayaashan waxaa la dhigaa dheri ay ku jiraan noocyo kala duwan oo dhir ah (tusaale ahaan, bocorka, digirta, gabbaldayaha, eggplant, cress, basbaaska, iwm.). Dareemayaashu waxay si joogto ah u ururiyaan xogta ku saabsan heerkulka, xoogga iftiinka, iyo qoyaanka ciidda.
 Barnaamijku wuxuu markaas abuuraa shiraac madhan oo cabbirkiisu yahay 100×100 pixels. Pixel kasta oo shiraacaan ku yaal waxaa lagu midabeeyaa iyadoo loo eegayo xogta laga helay dareemayaasha. Qiimaha RGB ee midabka pixel-ka waxaa lagu go'aamiyaa in qiimaha R (cas) uu u dhigmo heerkulka, G (cagaar) iftiinka, iyo B (buluug) qoyaanka.
 
Muuqaalka hal maalin
 Marka hore, waxaan eegaynaa nooc fudud oo mashruuca ah, halkaas oo hal sawir uu matalo hal maalin oo nolosha dhirta ah. Barnaamijku wuxuu bilaabmaa inuu midabeeyo pixel-ka ugu horreeya ee geeska bidix ee sare, wuxuuna si tartiib tartiib ah u midabeeyaa pixel kasta iyadoo loo eegayo qiyamka hadda jira ee laga helay dareemayaasha. Ka dib marka la midabeeyo pixel-ka, barnaamijku wuxuu sugaa in yar ka hor inta uusan u gudbin pixel-ka xiga. Marka uu gaaro dhamaadka safka, wuxuu u guuraa bilowga safka xiga wuxuuna sii wadaa midabaynta. Nidaamkani wuxuu socdaa ilaa dhammaan pixels-ka shiraaca la midabeeyo.

Isku-dubbaridka wax-soo-saarka
 Ka dib marka la muujiyo hal maalin, waxaan ballaarin karnaa nidaamkan si aan u abuurno taxane sawirro ah oo matalaya muddo dheer.
 Wax-soo-saarka labajibbaaran ee gaarka ah ayaa lagu dul dhejin karaa midba midka kale, taas oo abuuraysa sawirro isku dhafan. Maadaama mid cusub la bilaabo in la soo saaro isla marka mid hore la dhammeeyo, ma jirto wax kala go' ah marka loo eego socodka waqtiga. Sidan, waxaan ku abuuri karnaa sawirro leh xallin tusaale ahaan 100x500 pixels, haddii ay ka kooban yihiin shan wax-soo-saar.

Kordhinta dib u dhaca u dhexeeya cabbiraadaha
 Dib u dhaca u dhexeeya cabbiraadda pixels-ka shakhsi ahaaneed waa la kordhin karaa. Sidan, hal qaab oo labajibbaaran oo ah 100×100 pixels wuxuu matali karaa ma aha oo kaliya hal maalin, laakiin maalmo badan. Mabda'ani wuxuu noo ogolaanayaa inaan la soconno horumarka geedka muddo dheer hal sawir.

Isku-darka mabaadi'da
 Marka la isku daro labada mabda', waxaan abuuri karnaa taxane sawirro ah, halkaas oo sawir kasta uu matalo dhowr maalmood, taxanaha oo dhanna uu daboolo maalmo badan ama xitaa toddobaadyo. Sidan, waxaan ula socon karnaa oo u muujin karnaa horumarka muddada dheer ee geedka.

Fasiraadda natiijooyinka
 Sawirrada ka dhashay waxay na siinayaan aragti gaar ah oo ku saabsan nolosha dhirta. "Khadadka" buluugga ah ee sawirka waxay matalaan habeenkii, sababtoo ah inta badan way qoyanayd, laakiin ma ahayn mid aad u kulul, sidoo kale ma jirin iftiin. Taas bedelkeeda, "khadadka" jaalaha ah waxaa sabab u ah xaqiiqda ah in dhamaadka maalinta qorraxdu ay si toos ah ugu bilaabatay geedka, taas oo keentay korodhka heerkulka iyo xoogga iftiinka. Tani waxay ka muuqatay sawirka sida korodhka qiimaha kanaalka cas iyo cagaarka, kuwaas oo wadarta guud ay abuurayso midab jaalle ah. Mararka qaarkood, khadad daciif ah ayaa laga arki karaa bilowga habeenka, maadaama aan mararka qaarkood shido laambad qolka habeenkii. Wax-soo-saarka kale, waxaad ka arki kartaa hoos u dhaca xoogga iftiinka, tusaale ahaan, in roob da'ay.
 
Koodhka isha
 Koodhka Python ee mashruucan ayaa laga heli karaa halkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import serial
import pygame
import pygame.image
import decimal
import datetime

# Define constants
MAX_8BIT = 255
MAX_10BIT = 1023
WIDTH = 100
HEIGHT = WIDTH
SERIAL_PORT = 'COM3'
DELAY = 20 * 1000
MAX_RETRIES = 5
MIN_HUMIDITY = 750
MAX_HUMIDITY = 850

def actual_time():
    now_raw = str(datetime.datetime.now())
    now = now_raw.replace(":", "-")
    return now

def temperature_to_interval(temperature):
    return 1 / (1 + 2**(-1 * (temperature - 22)))

def remap_humidity(humidity, min_measured, max_measured):
    humidity_range = max_measured - min_measured
    humidity_proportion = (humidity - min_measured) / humidity_range
    lightness = int(humidity_proportion * MAX_10BIT)
    return min(max(lightness, 0), MAX_10BIT)

def max_255(v):
    return min(v, MAX_8BIT)

def remap_10bit_to_8bit(number):
    if not 0 <= number <= MAX_10BIT:
        raise ValueError("Input number must be between 0 and 1023")
    return round(number / 4)

def separate_data(data):
    temp, light, hum = map(float, data.split("|"))
    return temp, light, hum

def write_to_log(log):
    with open('log.txt', 'a') as f:
        f.write(log + "\n")

def read_arduino_data(ser, delay):
    data1 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data2 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data3 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    if len(data1) != len(data2) or len(data2) != len(data3):
        return read_arduino_data(ser, delay)
    return data1, data2, data3

ser = serial.Serial(port=SERIAL_PORT, baudrate=115200)

pygame.init()
win = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
surface = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
pygame.display.set_caption("TEMPERATURE + LIGHT + HUMIDITY")

for i in range(5):
    data_test = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(100)

x = 0
y = 0
img_num = 0
sensitivity = 1
start_time = actual_time()
running = True

while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            finish_time = actual_time()
            pygame.display.update()
            name = str(img_num)
            pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {} (incomplete).png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
            print("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            write_to_log("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            running = False
    if running == False:
        break

    for _ in range(MAX_RETRIES):
        try:
            data1, data2, data3 = read_arduino_data(ser, DELAY)
            break
        except Exception:
            print("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            write_to_log("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            pygame.time.wait(500)
    else:
        print("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        write_to_log("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        continue

    Rraw = (separate_data(data1)[0] + separate_data(data2)[0] + separate_data(data3)[0]) / 3
    R = round(max_255(temperature_to_interval(Rraw) * MAX_8BIT * sensitivity))

    Graw = (separate_data(data1)[1] + separate_data(data2)[1] + separate_data(data3)[1]) / 3
    G = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(Graw) * sensitivity))

    Braw = (separate_data(data1)[2] + separate_data(data2)[2] + separate_data(data1)[2]) / 3
    B = remap_humidity(Braw, min_measured=MIN_HUMIDITY, max_measured=MAX_HUMIDITY)
    B = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(B) * sensitivity))

    color = [R, G, B]
    pygame.draw.rect(win, color, [x, y, 1, 1])
    surface.set_at((x, y), (color[0], color[1], color[2]))
    if (x + 1) < WIDTH:
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 255, 0], [x + 1, y, 1, 1])
    pygame.display.flip()

    #   print current data with colored text
    if color[0] + color[1] + color[2] > 127:
        text_color = "0"
    else:
        text_color = "255"
    log = "COLOR: [{}, {}, {}],  TEMP.: {:.2f}°C, LIGHT: {}, HUM.: {},  TIME: {},  IMAGE NUMBER: {},   COMPLETED: {:.3f}%,  x: {}, y: {}" \
        .format(R, G, B, Rraw, round(Graw), round(Braw), actual_time(), img_num, (WIDTH * y + x + 1) / (WIDTH * HEIGHT) * 100, x, y)
    print('\033[38;2;{};{};{}m\033[48;2;{};{};{}m'
          .format(text_color, text_color, text_color, str(color[0]), str(color[1]), str(color[2])) +
          log +
          '\033[0m')
    write_to_log(log)

    x += 1
    if x >= WIDTH:
        x = 0
        y += 1
    if y >= HEIGHT:
        y = 0
        finish_time = actual_time()
        pygame.display.update()
        name = str(img_num)
        pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {}.png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
        print("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        write_to_log("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 0, 0], [0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT])
        surface.fill((0, 0, 0))
        img_num += 1
        start_time = actual_time()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
 
Gabagabo
 Mashruucani waa tusaale aad u wanaagsan oo ku saabsan sida aan u isticmaali karno tignoolajiyada si aan u fahamno oo aan u muujinno hababka dabiiciga ah. Sawirrada ka dhashay waxay na siinayaan aragti ku saabsan nolosha dhirta iyo kakanaanta kicinta la dareemay.
 
Waa muhiim in la ogaado in inkasta oo mashruucani uu bixiyo aragti gaar ah oo ku saabsan waayo-aragnimada dhirta, kuma jiraan dhammaan dhinacyada aragtidooda. Dhirtu waxay awood u leedahay inay dareento noocyo badan oo kala duwan oo calaamado ah oo ka imanaya deegaankooda, oo ay ku jiraan qoyaanka hawada, jihada cuf-jiidka, halabuurka kiimikada ee ciidda, goobta elektromagnetic-ka, iyo walbahaarka farsamada. Mashruucani ma isku dayayo inuu duubo waayo-aragnimada dhammaystiran ee geedka, laakiin wuxuu diiradda saarayaa saddex arrimood oo muhiim ah oo si fudud loo cabbiri karo loona muujin karo.
 
Ma hayno wax fikrad ah sida dhirtu u fasirto xogtan, laakiin waxaan ognahay inay hubaal ka falceliyaan. Mashruucu waa isku day lagu turjumayo waayo-aragnimadan qaab aan si fiican u fahmi karno oo aan u muujin karno.

TITLE: Soo-celinta barnaamijka MS Word
SLUG: rekurze-programu-ms-word
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/rekurze-programu-ms-word/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-11-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/rekurze-programu-ms-word.mdx
BUILT_HTML: rekurze-programu-ms-word/index.html
============================================================
Soo-celinta barnaamijka MS Word
 
 Noofambar 2022 
 
 
 
Waxaan helay hab lagu bilaabi karo habka soo-celinta (recursion) ee barnaamijka Microsoft Word. Waxaa loo baahan yahay laba shay – shaqada isku xirka iyo shaxda. Adigoo isticmaalaya shaqada isku xirka, waxaad gelin kartaa nuxurka dukumeenti A dukumeenti B si haddii nuxurka dukumeenti A la beddelo, isbeddelladaas ay si toos ah uga muuqdaan dukumeenti B. Si kale haddii loo dhigo – nuxurka dukumeenti B wuxuu tixraacayaa nuxurka dukumeenti A. Soo-celinta waxaa lagu bilaabi karaa iyadoo la abuurayo nuxur tixraacaya naftiisa. Sidee taas loo gaari karaa? Waxa kaliya ee loo baahan yahay waa in la abuuro dukumeenti A (aan u bixinno tusaale ahaan doc A.docx) iyo dukumeenti B (doc B.docx), dukumeenti A ku samee shax madhan, koobi garee, oo ku dheji dukumeenti B adigoo isticmaalaya isku xirka. Shaxdan cusub ee la geliyay ka dib waa in laga koobi gareeyaa dukumeenti B oo lagu dhejiyaa isku xir ahaan gudaha nuxurka shaxda ee dukumeenti A. Tani waxay abuuri doontaa shax si tartiib tartiib ah u bilaabi doonta inay nafteeda geliso. Inta badan, MS Word wuxuu bilaabi doonaa inuu hakado ilaa ugu dambeyntii uu gebi ahaanba xirmo. Nidaamkan oo dhan waxaa lagu arki karaa muuqaalka hoose.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xaE-eYQw4Mw]
