============================================================
TITLE: Caleenka geedka liin dhanaanta
SLUG: lipovy-list
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/lipovy-list/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-10-03
SOURCE: src/content/posts/lipovy-list.mdx
BUILT_HTML: lipovy-list/index.html
============================================================
Caleenka geedka liin dhanaanta
 
 Oktoobar 2019 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lipovy-list-obr-1.jpg | sawirka caleenta geedka liin dhanaanta]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lipovy-list-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lipovy-list-obr-2.jpg | naqshadda joometeriga ee caleenta geedka liin dhanaanta]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lipovy-list-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lipovy-list-obr-3.jpg | qeexidda rasmiga ah ee naqshadda joometeriga]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lipovy-list-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
Sawirka koowaad waa sawir fudud oo caleen geedka liin dhanaanta ah. Kan labaad waa qaab-dhismeedka joometeriga ee qaabka caleentan. Qaabka waxaa lagu dhisay oo kaliya iyadoo la adeegsanayo lix goobo iyo dhowr qaab oo kale oo joometeri ah oo caawiya. Waxaa xusid mudan in caleentan si fudud loo sawiri karo iyadoo la adeegsanayo oo kaliya qalin, saddex xagal leh xariiqyo, iyo qalabka goobada lagu sawiro (compass). Saamiga cabbirka ee qaababka joometeriga ee loo baahan yahay dhismaha ayaa ah kuwo aad u fudud. Tusaale ahaan, goobaha K3 iyo K4 waxay leeyihiin gacan laba jeer ka weyn kan goobaha K1 iyo K2. Sidoo kale, "jirridda" caleenta (oo loo yaqaan 'petiole' marka la eego dhinaca cilmiga) waxay leedahay dherer u dhigma gacan-qabsiga goobaha K3 iyo K4. Waxaa hubaal ah in la xuso saamiga u dhexeeya dhererka jirridda (xariiqda P2A) iyo caleenta lafteeda (xariiqda P2B). Waxay ku jiraan saamiga 1:1,6143, kaas oo ah qiyaas loogu talagalay saamiga dahabiga ah (golden ratio) [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zlat%C3%BD_%C5%99ez] oo sax ah ilaa laba meelood oo tobanle ah (φ≈1,6180…). Sawirka saddexaad waa isomorfik [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Izomorfismus] marka loo eego sawirka labaad (wuxuu hayaa dhammaan macluumaadka sawirka labaad, sidaas darteed waa matalaad kale oo shaygaas la mid ah). Waxaa lagu muujiyay calaamado sida loo sawiro caleentan. Caleenta geedka liin dhanaanta ayaa markaas lagu qeexay inay tahay urur ka kooban hal xariiq (jirridda) iyo dhowr qaanso (caleenta lafteeda).

============================================================
TITLE: Isku-dhaafyada tirooyinka koowaad
SLUG: prvociselne-spiraly
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/prvociselne-spiraly/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-10-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/prvociselne-spiraly.mdx
BUILT_HTML: prvociselne-spiraly/index.html
============================================================
Isku-dhaafyada tirooyinka koowaad
 
 Oktoobar 2019 
 
 
 
Mashruucan, waxaan isku dayay inaan helo qaababka isku-xigxiga xisaabta [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aritmetick%C3%A1_posloupnost] oo leh tirooyin koowaad oo aad u badan [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prvo%C4%8D%C3%ADslo]. Qaababkan waxaan ku helay anigoo abuuraya isku-dhaafyo tirooyin ah oo la mid ah isku-dhaafka Ulam [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ulamova_spir%C3%A1la] oo laga sameeyay saddex qaab oo joomatari ah - hexagons, labajibbaaran, iyo saddexagal. Waxaan doortay qaababkan sababtoo ah teselation-ka joogtada ah ee dusha sare [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teselace] waxaa lagu gaari karaa oo kaliya saddexdan qaab. Si kale haddii loo dhigo: qaababkan oo kaliya ayaa la isku dhejin karaa iyada oo aan wax farqi ah (meel bannaan) ka dhalan.
 
ISKU-DHAAFKA HEXAGON-KA
 Marka hore, waxaan sameeyay hal hexagon waxaana ku qoray lambar 1. Wareegga dibadda ee hexagon-kan oo dhan, waxaan ku sawiray hexagons kale waxaana ku qoray tirooyin dabiici ah oo togan jihada saacadda si lambarka 1 uu had iyo jeer ugu toosnaado hal tiir. Waxaan ku celceliyay habkan ilaa qaabku uu noqdo mid ku filan.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-1.jpg | tusaale habka loo abuurayo isku-dhaafka hexagon-ka]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-1.jpg] 
 
Ka dib, waxaan ku calaamadeeyay midab cagaaran dhammaan hexagons-ka ay ku jiraan tiro koowaad.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-2.jpg | isku-dhaafka hexagon-ka]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-2.jpg] 
 
Qaabkan, waxaa si lama filaan ah uga dhashay qaab cajiib ah. Waxaan ogaaday in labada tiir ee bidixda sare ay ku jiraan safaf tirooyin ah oo leh isku-xigxig xisaabeed, kaas oo farqigiisu yahay 6 (oo lagu muujiyay casaan), kuwaas oo ka kooban tirooyin koowaad oo aad u badan. Qaabka lagu xisaabinayo xubinta n-aad ee tiirka bidix waa a(n)=6*n+1 iyo tiirka midig a(n)=6*n+5. Waxaan ka raadiyay labadan isku-xigxig ee tirooyinka OEIS (Online Encyclopedia of Integer Sequences) waxaana ogaaday in labaduba ay horey ugu jireen xog-ururinta hoos yimaada koodhadhka A016921 [https://oeis.org/A016921] iyo A016969 [https://oeis.org/A016969]. Sidoo kale waxaan internetka ka helay sheegasho xiiso leh: qaybta isku-xigxiga a(n)=6n+1 iyo a(n)=6n+5 oo la isku daray waxay ka kooban yihiin dhammaan tirooyinka koowaad ee ka weyn 3. Marka laga reebo labadan tiir ee xiisaha leh ee ka kooban tirooyin koowaad oo badan, waxaan sidoo kale helay isku-xigxig tirooyin aan caadi ahayn (odd numbers) oo aan ka koobnayn wax tirooyin koowaad ah (oo lagu muujiyay buluug). Qaabka lagu xisaabinayo xubinta n-aad ee tiirka buluuga ah waa a(n)=6*n+3. Isku-xigxigan waxaan markii dambe ka helay xog-ururinta OEIS hoos yimaada koodhka A016945 [https://oeis.org/A016945].
 
ISKU-DHAAFKA LABAJIBBAARAN
 Si la mid ah, waxaan dhisay isku-dhaaf labajibbaaran, kaas oo ay sidoo kale ka soo muuqdeen saddexdan isku-xigxig.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-3.jpg | tusaale habka loo abuurayo isku-dhaafka labajibbaaran]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-3.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-4.jpg | isku-dhaafka labajibbaaran]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-4.jpg] 
 
ISKU-DHAAFKA SADDEXAGALKA
 Isku-dhaafkan, waxaa mar kale ka soo muuqday isku-xigxiga a(n)=6*n+1 iyo a(n)=6*n+5, laakiin waa la isku daray. Isku-darkan wuxuu dhacay sababtoo ah tallaabo kasta oo xitaa (even step) ah oo dhismaha isku-dhaafka ah, saddexagalka waxaa lagu wareejiyaa 180° (saddexagalada qaarkood waxay u jeedaan kor, kuwa kalena waxay u jeedaan hoos).
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-5.jpg | tusaale habka loo abuurayo isku-dhaafka saddexagalka]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-5.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-6.jpg | isku-dhaafka saddexagalka]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prvociselne-spiraly-obr-6.jpg]

TITLE: Dhalanteyska isbarbardhigga xariijimaha toosan iyo kuwa jiifka ah
SLUG: iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-08-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu/index.html
============================================================
Dhalanteyska isbarbardhigga xariijimaha toosan iyo kuwa jiifka ah
 
 Agoosto 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-1.jpg | isbarbardhigga xariijimaha toosan iyo kuwa jiifka ah]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-1.jpg] 
 
Xariijimaha jiifka ah ee sawirka sare ka muuqda ayaa u muuqda kuwo aan aad u kala duwanayn marka meel fog laga eego marka loo eego xariijimaha toosan. Dhab ahaantii, waa isku mid, sida laga arki karo marka meel dhow laga eego. Marka madaxa loo leexiyo bidix ama midig 45° dhalanteyskani wuu baaba'ayaa. Marka madaxa loo leexiyo bidix ama midig 90° xariijimaha jiifka ah waxay isu beddelaan kuwo toosan, sidaas darteedna waxay u muuqdaan kuwo aad u kala duwan. Dhalanteyskan waxaan ku dareemay sawirka Fibonacci spiral ee ereyga [https://vojtamaur.cz/spirala-fibonacciho-slova/]. Sawirkan labajibbaaran wuxuu u muuqdaa mid ka kooban afar saddexagal - laba ay ka buuxaan xariijimo jiif ah iyo laba ay ka buuxaan xariijimo toosan. Waxaan aaminsanahay in dhalanteyskani uu ku shaqeeyo mabda' la mid ah dhalanteyska toosan-jiifka ah [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vertical%E2%80%93horizontal_illusion]. Waxa kale oo aan aaminsanahay inay taasi la xiriirto falcelinta kooxo gaar ah oo neerfayaasha ah oo ku aaddan kicinta kala duwan. Neerfayaashu waxay si kala duwan uga falceliyaan kicinta kala duwan sida xariijimaha toosan, xariijimaha qaloocan, xariijimaha jiifka ah iwm (eeg buugga Gödel, Escher, Bach, cutubka 11). Waxa xusid mudan in dhalanteyskani uu qof walba si kala duwan u saameeyo - qof baa si cad u arka, qof kalena waxba kama saameeyo. Dhalanteyskani wuxuu xitaa uga sii muuqdaa sawirka hoose.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-2.jpg | sawir ka kooban xariijimo toosan iyo kuwa jiifka ah]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-2.jpg]

============================================================
TITLE: Spiralada ereyga Fibonacci
SLUG: spirala-fibonacciho-slova
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/spirala-fibonacciho-slova/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-08-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/spirala-fibonacciho-slova.mdx
BUILT_HTML: spirala-fibonacciho-slova/index.html
============================================================
Spiralada ereyga Fibonacci
 
 Agoosto 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-1.jpg | Spiralada ereyga Fibonacci: wax soo saarka barnaamijka Pavel Burda]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-1.jpg] 
 
Si aan u sawiro qaabkan, waxaan geliyay ereyga Fibonacci [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fibonacciho_slovo] spiralada si la mid ah sida tirooyinka dabiiciga ah loogu geliyo spiralada Ulam. Tirooyinka gaarka ah ee spiraladan waxaan ugu calaamadeeyay xeerkan: 0 = labajibbaaran madow, 1 = labajibbaaran cad (habkan waxaa lagu muujiyay animation-ka hoose). Si loo kordhiyo waxtarka habkan, waxaa la isticmaalay luuqadda qoraalka ee JavaScript.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-2.gif | muuqaal animation ah oo habka ah]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-2.gif] 
 
Waa maxay ereyga Fibonacci?
 Ereyga Fibonacci waa taxane gaar ah oo ka kooban lambaro binary ah (ama si guud ahaan laba calaamadood oo kale). Waxaan ku helnaa si la mid ah taxanaha Fibonacci [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fibonacciho_posloupnost], kaliya halkii aan ka isticmaali lahayn isugeyn, waxaan isticmaalnaa isku-xirka ereyada. Labada xubnood ee bilowga ah waa la qeexay: S0 = 0, S1 = 01. Xubinta xigta ee ka dhalata isku-xirka (taas oo ah, ku darista xubinta ka horreysa xubinta ugu dambeysa) waxay noqon doontaa S2 = 010. Taxanaha dhowrka lambar ee ugu horreeya ee ereyada Fibonacci waxay u ekaan lahaayeen sidan: 0100101001001010010100100101001001…
 
Waa maxay spiralada Ulam?
 Spiralada Ulam [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ulamova_spir%C3%A1la], ama sidoo kale loo yaqaan spiralada tirooyinka koowaad, waa sawir ka dhasha habaynta tirooyinka dabiiciga ah ee spiralada iyo muujinta tirooyinka koowaad. Waxaa helay xisaabyahan Stanisław Ulam sanadkii 1963. Ulam wuxuu qoray tirooyinka dabiiciga ah shabakad leydi ah, isagoo dhigay midka dhexe iyo tirooyinka kale oo u socda spiral ahaan dibadda.
 
Koodhka isha ee mashruuca
 
[CODE BLOCK]
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>Spirála Fibonacciho slova</title>

  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">

  <style>
    #container {
      text-align: center;
      position: absolute;
      left: 50%;
      top: 50%;
      padding: 20px;
      transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
      width: 600px;
      height: 600px;
      background-color: white;
    }

    #point {
      color: black;
      background-color: white;
      border:none;
      position: absolute;
      font-size:11px;
      text-align:center;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 10px;
      width:20px;
      height:20px;
    }

    #point2 {
      color: white;
      background-color: black;
      position: absolute;
      font-size:11px;
      text-align:center;
      border:none;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 10px;
      width:20px;
      height:20px;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <main id='container'>
  </main>

  <script>
    /* FUNKCE SPIRALY */
    function spiral(n) {
      if (n === 0) return [0, 0, r];
      --n;

      var r = Math.floor((Math.sqrt(n + 1) - 1) / 2) + 1;
      var p = (8 * r * (r - 1)) / 2;
      var en = r * 2;
      var a = (1 + n - p) % (r * 8);
      var pos = [0, 0, r];

      switch (Math.floor(a / (r * 2))) {
        case 0:
          {
            pos[0] = a - r;
            pos[1] = -r;
          }
          break;
        case 1:
          {
            pos[0] = r;
            pos[1] = (a % en) - r;
          }
          break;
        case 2:
          {
            pos[0] = r - (a % en);
            pos[1] = r;
          }
          break;
        case 3:
          {
            pos[0] = -r;
            pos[1] = r - (a % en);
          }
          break;
      }

      return pos;
    }

    /* FIBONACCIHO SLOVA */
    container = document.getElementById("container");
    var var0 = "<div id='point'></div>";
    var var1 = "<div id='point2'></div>";
    var var3;
    var text = "";
    var i;

    /* ITERACE NEFUNGUJE PO JEDNOM CTVERECKU ALE PO KAZDEM SOUCTU JEDNICEK A NUL */
    for (var i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
      var3 = var0 + var1;
      var1 = var0;
      var0 = var3;

      text += var3;
      container.innerHTML = text;
    }

    /* VOLANI FUNKCE SPIRALY */
    var cislo = document.getElementById('container').getElementsByTagName("div");

    for(var i=0; i<cislo.length; i++) {
      cislo[i].setAttribute("class", "jedno");

      var pos = spiral(i);

      cislo[i].style.right = 300 + 40 * pos[0] + "px";
      cislo[i].style.top = 300 + 40 * pos[1] + "px";
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Midab, qaab iyo dhawaaq
SLUG: barva-tvar-ton
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/barva-tvar-ton/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/barva-tvar-ton.mdx
BUILT_HTML: barva-tvar-ton/index.html
============================================================
Midab, qaab iyo dhawaaq
 
 cas 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png | aragtiyaha midabka - Wassily Kandinsky]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png] 
 
Sida uu qabo fannaanka Wassily Kandinsky [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vasilij_Kandinskij] iyo aragtidiisa midabka, waxaa jira xiriir ka dhexeeya midabka iyo qaabka. Qaab-dhismeedka midabka ee RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] (cas, jaalle, buluug) ee la isticmaali jiray xilligaas, wuxuu u qoondeeyay saddex 2D qaab oo joomatari ah xiriirkan: saddexagal = jaalle, laba jibbaaran = cas, goob = buluug. Waxaan ka dhiirigashaday shaqadiisa waxaana abuuray nooc casri ah oo ka mid ah aragtidan midabka. Halkii aan isticmaali lahaa qaab-dhismeedka midabka ee RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] oo laga yaabo inuu hadda duugoobay, waxaan isticmaalay qaab-dhismeedka midabka ee RGB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RGB] (cas, cagaar, buluug) oo dhanka fiisigiska ka saxsan oo maanta aad loo isticmaalo. Laba jibbaarkii uu isticmaalay Kandinsky waxaan ku beddelay shan-geesle, sababtoo ah laba jibbaarku wuxuu aad ugu eg yahay saddexagalka marka loo eego goobta, halka shan-geesluhu uu ii muuqdo mid ku habboon inuu noqdo kala-guurka u dhexeeya saddexagalka iyo goobta.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png | aragtiyaha midabka - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png] 
 
Si aan u helo qaab ku habboon (shan-geesle) oo u dhexeeya saddexagalka iyo goobta, waxaan isticmaalay xaglaha gudaha ee qaababka. Xagasha gudaha ee saddexagal siman waa 60° (ama 60^1). Xagasha gudaha ee goobtu waa inay noqotaa 180° (ama 60^1.268324336648371). Haa, goobtu ma laha dhinacyo, sidaas darteedna ma yeelan karto xaglo gudaha ah, laakiin tusaale ahaan, xagasha gudaha ee kun-geesluhu waa 179,64° qaabkanina wuxuu muuqaal ahaan aad ugu eg yahay goob. Haddii aan celcelis ahaan qaato labadan exponent 60^[(1+1.268324336648371):2] waxaan helayaa natiijo ah 103.923°. Xagashan gudaha ah ayaa ugu dhow shan-geesle, kaas oo xagasha gudahiisu ay tahay 108°. Si aan u ballaariyo aragtidan midabka, waxaan isla habraacan ku daray kala-guurka u dhexeeya qaababka midabka aasaasiga ah oo leh xiriiradan: laba jibbaaran = jaalle, sideed-geesle = buluug-cagaaran (cyan). Waxaan isku dayay inaan aragtidan ku dhammaystiro anigoo u qoondeynaya qaababka midabka leh dhawaaqa iyadoo loo eegayo xiriirka: xagal fiiqan + midab diirran = dhawaaq sarreeya, xagal furan + midab qabow = dhawaaq hooseeya. Fikradan waxaan ku duubay qoraalka muusigga.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png | aragtiyaha midabka ee la ballaariyay - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png] 
 
Waxaan isku dayay inaan aragtidan ku dhammaystiro anigoo u qoondeynaya qaababka midabka leh dhawaaqa iyadoo loo eegayo xiriirka: xagal fiiqan + midab diirran = dhawaaq sarreeya, xagal furan + midab qabow = dhawaaq hooseeya. Fikradan waxaan ku duubay qoraalka muusigga.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg | aragtiyaha muusigga midabka - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg] 
 
Midabka kaliya ee aanan ku guuleysan inaan u qoondeeyo qaab ama dhawaaq waa midabka guduudka ah (magenta). Waa inay la mid noqotaa cagaarka (sidoo kale waa wax u dhexeeya buluugga iyo casaanka, waana mid qabow oo diirran isku mar). Ma inay u ekaataa nus-goob? Mise saddexagal wareegsan? Mise inay la mid noqoto cagaarka - sidaas darteedna ay noqoto shan-geesle? Ilaa hadda, kama helin jawaab su'aashan.

============================================================
TITLE: Aragtida bini'aadamka
SLUG: lidsky-zrak
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/lidsky-zrak/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/lidsky-zrak.mdx
BUILT_HTML: lidsky-zrak/index.html
============================================================
Aragtida bini'aadamka
 
 Juun 2019 
 
 
 
Aragtida bini'aadamku dhab ahaan way ka duwan tahay waxa dadka badankoodu u haystaan. Xogta asalka ah ee ay indhaheenu soo saaraan aad bay uga duwan tahay sida maskaxdu noogu fasirto ka dib, sidaas darteedna waxa aynu u malaynayno in aynu aragno. Sawirka hoose waxa uu muujinayaa "xogta saafiga ah" oo ka madax bannaan "habaynta dambe" ee maskaxdeenu samayso.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png | muuqaalka aragtida bini'aadamka]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png] 
 
Sawirka waxa laga dareemi karaa dhawr cilladood oo muuqda:
 
 
- Sawirku waa mid dhinaca hore iyo gadaalba loo rogay. Ilmo kasta oo dhasha waxa uu u arkaa sidan, laakiin waqti ka dib maskaxdu waxay barataa inay sawirka saxdo. Saameyntan ayaa sidoo kale dhici karta marar aad u dhif ah ka dib marka la isticmaalo walxaha maskaxda beddela ee loo yaqaan dissociative hallucinogens.
 
- Sawirka waxa loo soo bandhigay il kasta si gaar ah - maskaxda oo keliya ayaa isku xidha hal sawir.
 
- Sawirku waa mid fiiqan oo keliya qayb yar oo dhexda ah, dhinacyadana wuu ku daboobayaa.
 
- Sawirku waa midka ugu midabka badan qaybta yar ee dhexda, dhinacyadana wuu ku luminayaa midabkiisa. Tani waxay u sabab tahay tirada badan ee unugyada midabka aragta (cones) ee bartamaha isha, kuwaas oo si degdeg ah hoos ugu dhaca marka laga fogaado bartamaha. Isha kasta waxa ku yaal hal dhibic oo madow - oo loo yaqaan bar indho la'aan ah (scotoma). Qaybtan isha kama jiraan wax unugyo ah oo midab ama iftiin aragta, sababtoo ah halkaas waxaa ku yaal dareemaha isha. Maskaxdu waxay caadi ahaan buuxisaa meelahan indho la'aanta ah iyadoo isticmaalaysa xogta isha kale.
 
- Khadadka toosan ayaa si fudud u qalloocan oo u eg qaansooyin, sida haddii ay saameyn ku yeelatay muraayadda kalluunka (fisheye) ama muraayad ballaadhan oo kamarad ah. Maskaxdu waxay isku daydaa inay toosiso khadadkan, laakiin waa dhalanteed keliya.
 
- Sanka ayaa sidoo kale ka muuqda goobta aragga, kaas oo maskaxdu ay caadi ahaan shaandhayso.
 
- Sawirku ma aha mid nadiif ah oo dhammaystiran, laakiin waxa uu ka kooban yahay buuq muuqaal ah oo daciif ah. Tani waxay ka dhalataa isku-darka buuqa jireed ee iftiinka, buuqa u gaarka ah unugyada iftiinka aragta, iyo hawlaha kale ee neerfaha ee nidaamka aragga. Xaaladaha caadiga ah, maskaxdu badanaa way caburisaa, laakiin waxay noqon kartaa mid muuqata gaar ahaan gudcurka, marka indhaha la xidho, ama meelaha siman.
 
 
Shaqadayda xiritaanka ee sannadkii labaad ee koorsada abuurista warbaahinta badan ee maaddada animation-ka ee Dugsiga Xirfadaha Ganacsiga, Farshaxanka Codsatay iyo Naqshadeynta, waxaan ku sameeyay muuqaal jilaya cillado kala duwan oo xiiso leh oo ku dhaca isha:
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/sTMWRaVGFIE]

Tanzania 2019
 
Sawirro, qoraallo
 
 
 
Bandhiggan waa soo koobidda indho-indhayn iyo aqoon kala duwan oo shakhsi ah oo aan ururiyey intii lagu jiray joogitaankaygii gaaban ee Tanzania. Indho-indhayntan iyo dareemadan waxaan ku muujinayaa sawirro iyo qoraal labadaba.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg] 

 
Waxaan isku dayayaa inaan ku caawiyo dadka deegaanka hal-abuurkayga, si aan u kordhiyo wacyiga ku saabsan dhibaatooyinkooda, taasina ay gacan ka geysato ciribtirka eexda jinsiyadeed ee qaarkood. Waxa kale oo aan aaminsanahay in Yurub ay tahay inay tusaale ka qaadato dalkan arrimo badan.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg] 
 
 
 
Tanzania waxaa ku nool laba kooxood oo waaweyn oo diimeed - Islaamka iyo Masiixiyadda. In kasta oo wada-noolaanshaha labadan kooxood ay u noqon karto qofka Yurubiyaanka ah ee caadiga ah ee qarnigii 21aad wax aan la qiyaasi karin, haddana Muslimiinta iyo Masiixiyiinta deegaanka ayaa halkan ugu nool si nabad ah oo ay isugu ixtiraamaan.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg] 

 
Shakhsi ahaan, ma jecli dadka xiran dareeska (booliska, askarta, ilaalada...). Ugu yaraan ma ahan Jamhuuriyadda Czech. Waxaan aaminsanahay in awoodaha la siiyay ay kordhiyaan kibirkooda. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, diidmadayda dareeska kuma aysan shaqayn si buuxda gudaha Tanzania opodstatněný.Lid dadka xiran dareeska halkaas waxay iila muuqdeen kuwo naxariis badan oo had iyo jeer ii gargaara.
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg] 

 
Dadka deegaanka ayaa iila muuqda kuwo aad u soo dhaweyn badan oo saaxiibtinimo leh, xitaa meelaha ka baxsan goobaha dalxiiska. Haddii aan booqdo tuulo maxalli ah, dadku waxay igu soo dhaweeyaan salaanta "Jambo!" (oo macnaheedu yahay "Hello!"). Qaar badan oo iyaga ka mid ah ayaa xitaa ila bilaabay sheeko.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg] 

 
Dadkan ka yimid gobollada kulul waxay iila muuqdaan kuwo dhoola cadeynaya in ka badan annaga (qayb ka mid ah wada hadal dhex maray rakaab dhowr ah oo aan maqlay diyaaradda markii aan ku soo laabanayay Jamhuuriyadda Czech)
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg] 
 
Mahadsanid caalamiyeynta, dalxiiska ayaa sidoo kale ka soo baxaya Zanzibar, kaas oo keena faa'iidooyin badan, laakiin sidoo kale khasaarooyin. Faa'iidooyinka ugu waaweyn waa koritaanka dhaqaalaha iyo heerka waxbarasho ee kor u kacaya. Faa'iidada kale waa kordhinta wacyiga dalkan ee adduunka, laakiin nasiib darro faa'iidadaas waxaa inta badan baabi'iya derbiyada waaweyn ee dhismayaasha hoteellada, kuwaas oo dalxiisayaal badani aysan waligood ka gudbin. Waxaan u arkaa burburinta dhaqanka maxalliga ah inay tahay khasaare. Tusaale ahaan arrintan waa socdaalka Masay badan oo ka yimid dhul-weynaha Tanzania (gaar ahaan aagga ku hareeraysan Kilimanjaro) ilaa meelaha ay ku badan yihiin dalxiisayaasha (xeebaha Zanzibar). Masay-gan ayaa halkaas ku iibiya alaabta qadiimiga ah waxayna u yihiin uun soo jiidasho dalxiis. Masay-gu waxay aaminsan yihiin in haddii qof sawiro, ay xadaan naftooda. Si kastaba ha ahaatee, meelaha dalxiiska, waxay u oggolaadaan in la sawiro dhowr senti.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]

Sawirka shakhsiyadda
 
 Diseembar 2018 
 
 
 
Mashruucan waxaa loo abuuray shaqo dugsi oo loogu talagalay maaddada Sawir-qaadista ee Dugsiga Sare ee Ganacsiga, Farshaxanka Codsatay iyo Naqshadeynta ee Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Ujeeddadu waxay ahayd in si tijaabo ah loo isku xiro sawir-qaadista dadka, noocyada shakhsiyadda cilmi-nafsiga, saadaalinta sawirka, animation-ka iyo codka. Qaabka ugu dambeeya ee sawirrada iyo fiidiyowyada waxay ku salaysnaayeen imtixaanka shakhsiyadda ee Eysenck iyo kala qaybinta dabeecadaha iyadoo loo eegayo afarta Galen.

TITLE: Qaabka shaxanka
SLUG: sachovnicovy-rastr
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/sachovnicovy-rastr/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/sachovnicovy-rastr.mdx
BUILT_HTML: sachovnicovy-rastr/index.html
============================================================
Qaabka shaxanka
 
 Diseembar 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lD_PCIbCvlk]
 
 
Waxaan sawirka tusaalaha ah ku dabaqay qaab shaxan ah, kaas oo berrin kasta oo shaxanka ka mid ahi ay leedahay cabbirka hal biiksely oo sawirka ah. Meelaha cad ee berrinka shaxanka ka dib marka la dabaqo qaabkan waa biikseliyadii asalka ahaa ee sawirka, halka meelaha madow ay yihiin kuwo midab ahaan iyo iftiin ahaanba lid ku ah. Sawirka oo cabbirkiisu buuxo wuxuu u muuqdaa midab cawlan oo siman, laakiin marka la soo dhoweeyo dhijitaal ahaan ayaa sawirka asalka ah la arki karaa. Si loogu soo bandhigo si sax ah kormeeraha Full HD, sawirku waa inuu lahaadaa qiyaastii 2000 biiksel oo dherer ah. Soo bandhigid khaldan ayaa dhici karta marka sawirka lagu furo biraawsar dib u habeyn ku sameeya shabagga biikseliyada iyadoo la adeegsanayo habka bicubic. Biraawsarka la xaqiijiyay waa tusaale ahaan Windows Photo Viewer.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/wehAxodpQGg]
 
 
Tusaale kale oo ku saabsan adeegsiga mabda'an, waxaan abuuray sawir ka kooban isbarbardhig midab iyo macno leh.

TITLE: Qalloocinta macluumaadka
SLUG: pokriveni-informace
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/pokriveni-informace/
LANGUAGE: so

SECTION: volna-tvorba

SOURCE: src/content/posts/pokriveni-informace.mdx

============================================================
Qalloocinta macluumaadka
 
 Agoosto 2018 
 
 
 
Immisa dhexeeye ayuu soo maraa maqaalka aan ku aragno internetka inta uusan na soo gaarin? Runnimadiisu ma tahay mid la aamini karo? Intee in le'eg ayuu dunida aan ku aragno shaashadaha taleefannadeenna, kombiyuutaradeenna iyo telefishannadeenna ka duwan yahay xaqiiqda?
 
Macluumaad kasta oo aan aragno waxaa muhiim ah in laga xaqiijiyo ilo badan oo aan lagu tiirsanayn runnimadiisa.
 
Shaqadan waxaa la abuuray iyadoo la qaaday hal sawir ka dibna si isdaba joog ah loo sawiray isla sawirkaas oo laga soo qaaday shaashadda kombiyuutarka.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-3.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-4.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-4.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-5.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-5.jpg]

TITLE: Laba laxan
SLUG: dva-rytmy
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/dva-rytmy/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/dva-rytmy.mdx
BUILT_HTML: dva-rytmy/index.html
============================================================
Laba laxan
 
 Abriil 2018 
 
 
 
Fiidiyowga koowaad wuxuu ka kooban yahay laba laxan: biligga muuqaalka iyo gujinta maqalka. Labadan laxan waxay leeyihiin muddooyin wax yar ka duwan. Kala duwanaanshaha yar awgeed, fiidiyowgu wuxuu gaaraa heer sare oo walwal ah qayb gaar ah. Fiidiyowga labaad, waxaan fikraddan aasaasiga ah ku daray agab muuqaal ah oo diiradda saaraya mawduuca ka hortagga dagaalka.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/X9ms8pp89Pg?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg | fikradda aasaasiga ah]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg] 
Laba laxan 1 - madow x caddaan, fikradda aasaasiga ah
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/MQIDzwTYjoA?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg | hirgelinta fikradda aasaasiga ah]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg] 
Laba laxan 2 - hirgelinta fikradda aasaasiga ah ee agabka muuqaalka

TITLE: Tiroob aan kala sooc lahayn oo uu doortay bini'aadam
SLUG: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/index.html
============================================================
Tiroob aan kala sooc lahayn oo uu doortay bini'aadam
 
 Abriil 2018 
 
 
 
Shaqadan, su'aalo ayaa loo qaybiyay dad dhowr ah. Dadka la tijaabiyay waxay su'aalahan ku qoreen boqol tiro oo aan kala sooc lahayn oo u dhexeeya 1-100, kuwaas oo maskaxdooda ku soo dhacay. Ka dib, waxaan xogtan u wareejiyay kombuyuutarka oo waxaan ka sameeyay garaaf mid kasta oo ka mid ah su'aalaha. Dhidibka jiifka ah ee x ayaa go'aaminaya siday u kala horreeyaan tirooyinka gaarka ah, dhidibka toosan ee y-na wuxuu go'aaminayaa qiimaha tirooyinka la doortay. Garaafka midabka buluugga ah waxaa sameeyay kombuyuutar, taas oo ah, matoor tirooyin aan kala sooc lahayn. Marka la barbar dhigo dhammaan garaafyada, waxaa la ogaan karaa in qof kasta oo la tijaabiyay uu leeyahay garaaf gaar ah oo leh hab u gaar ah oo lagu geliyo tirooyinka. Waxa kale oo la ogaan karaa in garaafyo badan ay ku celceliyaan wareegyo gaar ah oo ku dhow inay isku mid yihiin (badanaa qaab kor u kac iyo hoos u dhac ah oo tirooyinka ah).
 
TIROOYINKA UU DOORTAY MATOORKA TIROOYINKA AAN KALA SOOC LAHAYN
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg | Garaafka tirooyinka aan kala sooc lahayn ee uu doortay kombuyuutarku]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg]

TITLE: Neufurniin aan joogto ahayn
SLUG: nepravidelny-dech
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/nepravidelny-dech/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nepravidelny-dech.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nepravidelny-dech/index.html
============================================================
Neufurniin aan joogto ahayn
 
 Maarso 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/khCWjid0QeU?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
Qaybtan codka ah ee afar daqiiqo soconaysa waxaa ku jira duubitaan bisad dhimanaysa. Duubitaanka waxaa laga maqlayaa laba dhawaaq oo waaweyn: neufurniin aan joogto ahayn iyo dhawaaqa garaaca wadnaha oo aan sidaas u cadayn.

TITLE: Laga soo bilaabo aragtida qolofka
SLUG: z-pohledu-musle
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/z-pohledu-musle/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/z-pohledu-musle.mdx
BUILT_HTML: z-pohledu-musle/index.html
============================================================
Laga soo bilaabo aragtida qolofka
 
 Maarso 2018 
 
 
 
Fiidiyowyadan waxay diiradda saarayaan aragtida qolofka, maxay dareemaysaa oo ay la kulmaysaa? Aragtideeda adduunka kama muhiimsana midka bini'aadamka! Fiidiyowga koowaad, waxaa lagu arki karaa duubistii asalka ahayd ee badda. Fiidiyowga labaad, dhinaca bidix ee shaashadda waxaa lagu soo bandhigayaa celceliska midabka ee qaab-dhismeed kasta oo ka mid ah duubistii asalka ahayd. Dhinaca midig ee shaashadda waxaa ku yaal garaafka RGB ee qeexaya midabkan.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/mxfAcE8KYaQ?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lZknZa20ilY?rel=0&showinfo=0]

Caaryo
 
 Noofambar 2017 
 
 
 
Wareeggan sawirrada ah ayaa diiwaangelinaya caaryada ku jirta buuggeyga af Jarmalka. Sawirrada waxaa loo habeeyey siday u kala horreeyaan bogagga buugga. Tani waxay keenaysaa in sawir kasta uu yeesho qaab-dhismeed aan kala sooc lahayn, xiiso leh, iyo tijaabo ah.

TITLE: Qalalaasaha shabagga joogtada ah

SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs

SOURCE: src/content/posts/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru.mdx
BUILT_HTML: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/index.html
============================================================
Qalalaasaha shabagga joogtada ah
 
 Oktoobar 2017 
 
 
 
Shaqadan, waxaan isku dayay inaan abuuro taxane shaqooyin aan la taaban karin. Dukumeentiga waxaan u qaybiyay afar qaybood. Qaybta koowaad, waxaan abuuray shabag aasaasi ah oo joogto ah, qaybta labaad waxaan ka bilaabay shabaggii oo aan bilaabay inaan si yar u qalloociyo, taas oo keentay in la dareemo raaxo la'aan. Qaybta saddexaad, waxaan ku qalloociyay shabaggii joomatari ee joogtada ahaa qaabab dabiici ah oo aan joogto ahayn oo iska soo horjeeda. Qaybta afraad ee ugu dambaysana, waxaan ku sameeyay qaababkan dabiiciga ah animation.
 
Qaybta koowaad:
 SHABAGGA AASAASIGA AH
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg | Abstraction 1.0 - shabag joogto ah]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg] 




 
 
Qaybta labaad:
 QALALAASAHA SHABAGGA OO AY SABABTAY QALOOC YAR
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-2.jpg | Abstraction 2.0.0 - shabag joogto ah oo uu qalloociyay hal xariijin oo wareegsan]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-3.jpg | Abstraction 2.0.1 - shabag joogto ah oo ay qalloociyeen laba xariijin oo wareegsan]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-3.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-4.jpg | Abstraction 2.0.2 - shabag joogto ah oo ay qalloociyeen saddex xariijin oo wareegsan]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-4.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-5.jpg | Abstraction 2.1.0 - shabag joogto ah oo uu qalloociyay hal xariijin oo aan joogto ahayn]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-5.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-6.jpg | Abstraction 2.1.1 - shabag joogto ah oo ay qalloociyeen laba xariijin oo aan joogto ahayn]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-6.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-7.jpg | Abstraction 2.1.2 - shabag joogto ah oo ay qalloociyeen saddex xariijin oo aan joogto ahayn]

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-8.jpg | Abstraction 2.2.0 - shabag joogto ah oo uu qalloociyay hal xariijin oo aan joogto ahayn]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-8.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-9.jpg | Abstraction 2.2.1 - shabag joogto ah oo ay qalloociyeen laba xariijin oo aan joogto ahayn]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-9.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-10.jpg | Abstraction 2.2.2 - shabag joogto ah oo ay qalloociyeen saddex xariijin oo aan joogto ahayn]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-10.jpg] 
 
 
Qaybta saddexaad:
 QALALAASAHA SHABAGGA OO AY SABABEEN QAABAB DABIICI AH

Celcelkaabka midabka
 
 Abriil 2017 
 
 
 
Qaybta bidix ee sawirka waxaa ku yaal 1156 sawirro oo ku jira shabag 34x34 px ah. Pixel kasta oo midab leh wuxuu matalaa midabka celceliska ah ee sawir kasta oo ka yimid waddan la siiyay. Qaybta midig ee sawirka waa midabka celceliska ah ee dhammaan pixels-ka qaybta bidix, taas oo ah, midabka celceliska ah ee dhammaan sawirrada waddankaas.

TITLE: Xabadaha sonkorta
SLUG: kostky-cukru
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/kostky-cukru/
LANGUAGE: so

SECTION: volna-tvorba

BUILT_HTML: kostky-cukru/index.html
============================================================
Xabadaha sonkorta
 
 Maarso 2017 
 
 
 
Taxanaha sawirrada waxaa la qaaday intii lagu jiray imtixaanadii hibada ee Dugsiga Sare ee Ganacsiga, Farshaxanka Codsiga iyo Nashqadaynta ee Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Qaybta sawir-qaadista ee imtixaanada, waxaan awoodnay inaan keeno walxo noo gaar ah, laakiin tiradooda iyo cabbirkooduba way xaddidnaayeen. Waxaan keenay sanduuq xabadaha sonkorta ah, kuwaas oo aan ku diyaariyey saxan qaab shabag ah ka dibna aan biyo ku shubay. Xabadaha ayaa si tartiib tartiib ah u milmay, u burburay, oo si aan kala sooc lahayn u dhacay.

TITLE: Maktabadda farshaxanka waddooyinka
SLUG: street-art-knihovna
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/street-art-knihovna/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/street-art-knihovna.mdx
BUILT_HTML: street-art-knihovna/index.html
============================================================
Maktabadda farshaxanka waddooyinka
 
 Maarso 2017 
 
 
 
Meel kasta oo na hareeraysan waxaa ka buuxa farshaxanno waddooyin oo xiiso leh, laga bilaabo istiikarada yaryar, darbiyada la rinjiyeeyay ilaa noocyada kala duwan ee 3D walxaha. Maadaama qorayaasha farshaxannadan ay tahay inay ku sii jiraan magac-la'aan sababo la xiriira khilaafka sharciga, dunidan farshaxanka waddooyinka waa mid gebi ahaanba aan danaysi lahayn oo ka go'an dunida ganacsiga. Farshaxannada waddooyinka ee aan ku arki karno meel kasta oo magaalada ka mid ah ayaa badanaa la hayaa iyagoo aad u cusub. Farshaxanno waddooyin oo cusub ayaa daboolaya kuwii hore, rinjigu wuu ka diirayaa darbiyada, walxaha 3D ayaa la kulma falal waxyeellayn ah, istiikaraduna waxay ka diirmaan ama midabkoodu baaba'aa waqti ka dib saamaynta deegaanka awgeed. Waa hab dabiici ah, dhinac ka mid ah waqtiga, si kastaba ha ahaatee, waxaan aaminsanahay in qaar ka mid ah farshaxannada waddooyinka ay mudan yihiin in noloshooda la dheereeyo.
 
Taasina waa sababta aan u go'aansaday inaan diiwaangeliyo shaqooyinka ugu xiisaha badan. Ujeedadan awgeed, sanadkii 2017 waxaan aasaasay bog Facebook ah oo lagu magacaabo Maktabadda farshaxanka waddooyinka, kaas oo aan ilaa hadda ku daro qoraallo. Si aan u gaaro dad badan, waxaan markaas ka dib aasaasay bog isku magac ah oo ku yaal Instagram.
 
Bogaggan waxaan isku dayaa inaan ku daro inta badan shaqooyinka xambaarsan fikrad gaar ah, qaybta bilicsanaanta ee shaqadana waxaan u arkaa mid labaad.
 
Maktabadda farshaxanka waddooyinka (Facebook):
 www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna]
 
Maktabadda farshaxanka waddooyinka (Instagram):
 www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg | Sawirka shaashadda ee bogga Facebook]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg | Sawirka shaashadda ee bogga Instagram]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg]

Muhiim ma aha gobolka aad ka timid...
 
 11. 7. 2016 - 14. 8. 2016, Plzeň

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
 
28. Diseembar 2014 ayaan Mombasa ku sawiray darbi duug ah oo wax lagu qoray. Kadib waxaan isku dayay inaan fahmo qoraalka ku qoran. Qayb ka mid ah oo la akhriyi karay ayaan galiyay mashiinka raadinta internetka, sidaas ayaanan ku helay sawirka soo socda.
 

 

It doesn't matter which religion you claim you're. It doesn't matter which country you're coming from. It doesn't matter whether you 're poor or rich. It doesn't matter if you're black or white. We are all the same in the eyes of God.

 

 

Nezáleží, jakého jsi vyznání. Nezáleží, z jaké země pocházíš. Nezáleží, jestli jsi chudý nebo bohatý. Nezáleží, jestli jsi černoch nebo běloch. Všichni jsme si rovni v očích Boha.

 
 
 
 
Warbixin ka timid Plzeň TV:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jxg-6iVPPmk

Dusha
 
 4. 2. 2016 - 3. 4. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 Matxafka Magaalada Plzeň: KA HOR, HADA, KA DIB 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/GALBEnzekBw?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
Intee ayay noloshayada internetka iyo noloshayada dhabta ahi kala duwan yihiin? Maxaan u bannaanka u baxnaa haddii aan wax walba ka heli karno Google? Macluumaadka internetka waa kuwo qalloocan oo xiriir yar la leh xaqiiqda.
 Waa milicsiga waxa aan rabno inaan aragno.
 Qorraxdu waxay ka dhex muuqataa dusha biyaha, waxay ku soo dhacdaa derbiga waxayna ka dhex muuqataa muraayadda… Ma run ahaantii ayay noo soo gaartay iyadoo aan waxba iska beddelin?

TITLE: Laga bilaabo magaalada ilaa nasiibka
SLUG: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/
LANGUAGE: so
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2016-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/index.html
============================================================
Laga bilaabo magaalada ilaa nasiibka
 
 Janaayo 2016 
 
 
 
Sannadkii 2016 anigoo qayb ka ah diyaarinta dugsiga sare ee farshaxanka ee ZUŠ Plzeň, Jagellonská 14 waxaa laygu amray inaan sameeyo qaabayn garaaf ah oo magaalada ah. Waxaan xiisaynayay ilaa heerka ay qaabayntani gaari karto, sidaas darteed waxaan ka shaqaynayay hawshan sannadihii xigay ilaa hadda (sannadkii 2019). Laga bilaabo qaabayn fudud oo magaalada ah, waxaan u gudbay carqaladaynta shabakadaha joogtada ah, taas oo dhinaca farshaxanka ka dhalisay xiisad la dareemi karo. Intaas ka dib, waxaan isku dayay inaan hawshan u adeegsado nasiibka, laakiin ma aanan awoodin inaan helo wax u dhigma nasiib dhab ah oo aan lahayn sabab. Determinism-ka ayaa mar walba i hor taagnaa, sidaas darteed waxaan isticmaalay oo kaliya pseudo-nasiib (sababta waa la go'aamin karaa, laakiin natiijada way adagtahay in la saadaaliyo, sidaas darteed waxay u muuqataa mid nasiib ah). Qaar ka mid ah waddooyinka ayaa i geeyay meel aan waxba ka soo baxayn, sidaas darteed waa inaan raadiyaa aragti kale iyo xal garaaf oo kale, tusaale ahaan barnaamijka wax soo saarka muuqaalka, saamaynta moiré, taxanaha Fibonacci, iwm. Taxanaha sawirada ee hoose waa kooban ee shaqooyinkan.

============================================================
Haramo
 
 Maajo 2008 
 
 
 
Shaqo laga keenay xannaanada carruurta.
 
Hawshu waxay ahayd in laga gooyo warqad midab leh ubaxyo ubaxaya oo laga sameeyo sawir.
 
Halkii aan ka samayn lahaa ubaxyo, waxaan sameeyay plevel.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg | plevel]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg]
